```bash
remote_main=$(libra rev-parse origin/main)
test -n "$remote_main"
base_version=$(version_of "$remote_main")
test -n "$base_version"
new_version=$(printf '%s\n' "$base_version" | awk -F. '{ printf "%d.%d.%d\n", $1, $2, $3 + 1 }')
vtmp=$(mktemp -d /Volumes/Data/tmp/vsurf.XXXXXX)
trap 'rm -rf "$vtmp"' EXIT
for f in Cargo.toml Cargo.lock install.sh install.ps1; do
  libra cat-file -p "$remote_main:$f" > "$vtmp/base"
  if [ "$VSRC" = head ]; then libra cat-file -p "HEAD:$f" > "$vtmp/new"; else cp "$f" "$vtmp/new"; fi
  changes=$(diff "$vtmp/base" "$vtmp/new" | grep '^[<>]' || true)
  if [ "${MODE:-normal}" = repair ]; then
    want=
  else
    case "$f" in
      Cargo.toml|Cargo.lock) want=$(printf '< version = "%s"\n> version = "%s"' "$base_version" "$new_version") ;;
      install.sh) want=$(printf '< DEFAULT_VERSION="v%s"\n> DEFAULT_VERSION="v%s"' "$base_version" "$new_version") ;;
      install.ps1) want=$(printf '< $DefaultVersion = "v%s"\n> $DefaultVersion = "v%s"' "$base_version" "$new_version") ;;
    esac
  fi
  if [ "$changes" != "$want" ]; then echo "unexpected version-surface change in $f" >&2; exit 1; fi
done
echo "version surfaces OK (${MODE:-normal}): base $base_version"
```

**ER-05 审查摘要（`bash` 运行，`CARD` 为本卡 ID；有 C 组提交的卡在 ER-05 PASS 后运行，输出写成 `<CARD>:ER05_REVIEWED=` 的值）：** 对工作区里本卡的产品改动取摘要：逐个列出未提交与未跟踪的路径（`--untracked-files=all`，目录展开到文件），去掉本文件、`plan-status.md` 与四个版本面文件，按路径排序后写成「内容 SHA-256 与路径」一行一个（已删除的写 `deleted`），再取整体 SHA-256。没有产品改动的卡（如只做版本承载的发布卡）得到空集合的摘要 `e3b0c44298fc1c149afbf4c8996fb92427ae41e4649b934ca495991b7852b855`。暂存守卫 `PHASE=c` 内含同一函数。

```bash
set -eo pipefail
cd /Volumes/Data/gitmono/libra
plan=docs/development/plan/issues/1055.md
status_file=docs/development/plan/plan-status.md
review_digest() {
  libra status --porcelain --untracked-files=all | awk 'length($0) > 3 { print substr($0, 4) }' | sort | while IFS= read -r path; do
    case "$path" in "$plan"|"$status_file"|Cargo.toml|Cargo.lock|install.sh|install.ps1) continue ;; esac
    if [ -e "$path" ]; then printf '%s %s\n' "$(shasum -a 256 "$path" | cut -d' ' -f1)" "$path"; else printf 'deleted %s\n' "$path"; fi
  done | shasum -a 256 | cut -d' ' -f1
}
review_digest
```

**精确句预检（`bash` 运行，有网站面的卡在提交前执行）：** 三份仓库文档各自至少有一行恰好包含本卡精确句，且这一行在围栏代码块之外（代码块经语法高亮后会被拆成多个 `span`，网站段三按原始 HTML 匹配的检查就找不到它）；句子不能折行，也不能加反引号或其它行内标记。本卡承诺在 `COMPATIBILITY.md` 某行内追加精确句的（`case` 里的 `compat_sent`），该行也必须含精确句。

```bash
set -eo pipefail
cd /Volumes/Data/gitmono/libra
case "${CARD:?export CARD first}" in
  I1055-01) CMDS='rev-parse cat-file'; sentence='Colons inside a ^{/pattern} search are not path separators.'; compat_sent='217 265' ;;
  I1055-02) CMDS='show'; sentence='In show, colons inside a ^{/pattern} search are not path separators, and a :/pattern search names a commit, so format.pretty applies as in Git.'; compat_sent='199' ;;
  I1055-03) CMDS='ls-tree'; sentence='In ls-tree, colons inside a ^{/pattern} search are not path separators, and a ^{/pattern} search that matches no commit fails instead of resolving to a different commit.'; compat_sent='203' ;;
  *) echo "no website sentence for CARD=$CARD" >&2; exit 1 ;;
esac
for command in $CMDS; do
  for doc in "docs/commands/${command}.md" "docs/commands/zh-CN/${command}.md" "docs/development/commands/${command}.md"; do
    outside=$(awk -v s="$sentence" 'index($0, "\140\140\140") == 1 { fence = !fence; next } !fence && index($0, s) { n++ } END { print n + 0 }' "$doc")
    test "$outside" -ge 1
  done
done
for n in $compat_sent; do
  test "$(sed -n "${n}p" COMPATIBILITY.md | grep -cF "$sentence")" -ge 1
done
```

**暂存守卫（`bash` 运行，`PHASE` 取 `start`、`accept`、`c`、`amend`、`remote`、`terminal`、`blocked` 或 `abandon`）：** 暂存区开始时必须为空；工作区里的改动路径都必须在该 `PHASE` 允许留在工作区的集合内；`PHASE=c` 与 `PHASE=amend` 时再核对 C 组快照未变，并要求 C 组通过标记与从本文件重新抽出的脚本一致；`PHASE=c` 另要求「实施证据」有本卡的四行 ER-05 收据（每个键恰好一行，`ER05_VERDICT=PASS`），且 `ER05_REVIEWED` 等于在当前树上重算的 ER-05 审查摘要；`amend` 时要么运行器已在重放后的树上重跑过（快照与通过标记都对应当前树），要么有 C 组结论携带检查写下的携带标记（见该检查：上游增量只含不被测试与构建读取的计划文件，标记指向当前 `HEAD`、绑定当前通过标记），守卫会重新核对携带条件；卡字段的转移按 `PHASE` 核对（读 `HEAD` 与工作区的本文件比较）：`start` 只能 `pending` / 空 → `in-progress` / 空，`accept` 只能 `in-progress` / 空 → `in-progress` / `locally-accepted`，`c` 只能写 `locally-accepted` 或保持 `locally-accepted`、`remote-pending`（修复与前滚），`amend` 不改，`remote` 只能 → `remote-pending`（修复后的再次登记保持不变），`terminal` 只能 `in-progress` / `remote-pending` → `done` / `complete`，`blocked` 只能在 `in-progress` 与 `blocked` 之间切换且 `Acceptance` 等于 `HEAD` 已提交的值（工作区里未提交的 `locally-accepted` 要先改回），`abandon` 只能从 `in-progress` 或 `blocked`（`Acceptance` 为空或 `locally-accepted`）回到 `pending` / 空，且状态文件没有 `PUSHED_SHA`；同一提交里其它卡的字段都不得改变（本计划登记的联动卡在 `remote`、`terminal` 时与本卡同步转移）。每个 `PHASE` 都要求 `plan-status.md` 里本计划行在工作区与 `HEAD` 各恰好一行，并且要暂存的路径里没有冲突标记行（`<<<<<<< `、`||||||| `、`>>>>>>> ` 开头或恰为 `=======` 的行）；`abandon` 另向 GitHub 核对第 9 步列出的「没有推送过」条件。`remote` 还要求「实施证据」有本卡的 `<CARD>:C_COMMIT=`、`<CARD>:PUSHED_SHA=`、`<CARD>:VERSION=` 行且与状态文件一致，并向 GitHub 确认该 SHA 在远端 `main` 上、候选分支指向它；`terminal` 要求本卡的 `CI_BASE_RUN`、`CI_CODEQL_RUN`、`TAG`、`RELEASE_RUN`、`CANDIDATE_DELETED`、`ROLLBACK_VERIFIED`（有网站面时另加 `SITE_SHA`、`SITE_VERIFIED` 与 `SITE_REVIEWED`，并要求 `SITE_REVIEW_SESSION`、`SITE_REVIEW_OUTPUT_SHA256`、`SITE_REVIEW_VERDICT=PASS` 与 `SITE_REVIEWED` 各恰好一行）各行与状态文件一致，并向 GitHub 重新核对：tag 指向 `PUSHED_SHA`，两个候选分支 run 与 release run 都是 success 且 head SHA 是 `PUSHED_SHA`，五个 release job 全部 success，候选分支已删除；有网站面的卡还要求 `<CARD>:SITE_VERIFIED=` 行（网站段三确认两个线上页面含精确句后写入），并重新取回 EN 与 zh-CN 两个线上页面确认含精确句、后端 `origin/cf` 包含 `SITE_SHA`；`accept` 从本文件抽出本卡的门块重跑一遍；`COMPATIBILITY.md` 的 hunk：本卡第一次 C 组提交（状态文件还没有 `PUSHED_SHA`）必须改登记的行，之后的修复或前滚提交可以不改，改了仍按下述单行替换核对；`plan-status.md` 的未暂存差异按 hunk 解析，每个增删行（含空行与以 `-`/`+` 开头的内容行）都必须是本计划行；`COMPATIBILITY.md` 的每个 hunk 只能是本卡登记行号上的单行替换（`-n,1 +n,1`），且该行在 `HEAD` 与工作区都是预期的命令行；共享索引文件（如有登记）的差异行都必须以本卡的行前缀开头且增删行数相等；暂存后已暂存路径必须全部在该 `PHASE` 的暂存集合内。`start`、`remote`、`terminal` 只允许并暂存本文件与 `plan-status.md`；`accept` 同样只允许这两个文件，且只用于没有 C 组提交的卡；`blocked` 只暂存这两个文件，但允许本卡写集内未提交的改动留在工作区；`abandon` 只允许并暂存这两个文件（本卡的其它改动必须先撤销）；`amend` 只允许本文件（rebase 恢复后更新 C 组收据，之后执行 `libra commit --amend -S --no-edit`：重放保留了原提交信息里的 `Signed-off-by`，再加 `-s` 会写出第二行，2026-11-07 已实测；`plan-status.md` 会被测试读取，C 组之后不再改动）。版本面文件的内容由版本面内容检查约束。任一检查失败即停止，不提交。

```bash
set -eo pipefail
cd /Volumes/Data/gitmono/libra
plan=docs/development/plan/issues/1055.md
status_file=docs/development/plan/plan-status.md
state_dir=/Volumes/Data/tmp/libra-plan-state
case "${CARD:?export CARD first}" in
  I1055-01) allowed='src/utils/util.rs tests/command/cat_file_test.rs tests/command/rev_parse_test.rs docs/commands/rev-parse.md docs/commands/zh-CN/rev-parse.md docs/development/commands/rev-parse.md docs/commands/cat-file.md docs/commands/zh-CN/cat-file.md docs/development/commands/cat-file.md COMPATIBILITY.md'; compat_lines='217 265'; c_phase=yes; row_file=''; row_prefix='' ;;
  I1055-02) allowed='src/command/show.rs tests/command/show_test.rs docs/commands/show.md docs/commands/zh-CN/show.md docs/development/commands/show.md COMPATIBILITY.md'; compat_lines='199'; c_phase=yes; row_file=''; row_prefix='' ;;
  I1055-03) allowed='src/command/ls_tree.rs tests/command/ls_tree_test.rs docs/commands/ls-tree.md docs/commands/zh-CN/ls-tree.md docs/development/commands/ls-tree.md COMPATIBILITY.md'; compat_lines='203'; c_phase=yes; row_file=''; row_prefix='' ;;
  *) echo "unknown CARD=$CARD" >&2; exit 1 ;;
esac
compat_prefix() {
  case "$1" in
    199) printf '%s\n' '| show | partial | object/commit display,' ;;
    203) printf '%s\n' '| ls-tree | partial | Commit/tree listing, recursive listing' ;;
    217) printf '%s\n' '| rev-parse | partial | strict revision/object parsing' ;;
    265) printf '%s\n' '| cat-file | partial |' ;;
    *) return 1 ;;
  esac
}
check_compat_rows() {
  local file="$1" n prefix hits
  for n in $compat_lines; do
    prefix=$(compat_prefix "$n")
    hits=$(grep -nF -- "$prefix" "$file" | cut -d: -f1 | tr '\n' ' ')
    if [ "$hits" != "$n " ]; then echo "COMPATIBILITY.md row $n is not the expected row in $file (prefix found at: $hits)" >&2; return 1; fi
  done
}
STATE_DIR=/Volumes/Data/tmp/libra-plan-state
STATE="$STATE_DIR/1055-${CARD:?export CARD first}.env"
state_get() { if [ -f "$STATE" ]; then awk -v k="$1" 'index($0, k "=") == 1 { v = substr($0, length(k) + 2) } END { print v }' "$STATE"; fi; }
state_put() { mkdir -p "$STATE_DIR"; touch "$STATE"; awk -v k="$1" 'index($0, k "=") != 1' "$STATE" > "$STATE.tmp"; printf '%s=%s\n' "$1" "$2" >> "$STATE.tmp"; mv "$STATE.tmp" "$STATE"; }
on_remote_main() {
  local resp code rel
  set +e
  resp=$(gh api "repos/libra-tools/libra/compare/$1...main" -i 2>/dev/null)
  set -e
  code=$(printf '%s\n' "$resp" | awk 'NR == 1 { print $2 }')
  case "$code" in
    200) rel=$(printf '%s\n' "$resp" | sed -n '/^{/,$p' | jq -er .status) || return 1 ;;
    404) printf '%s\n' no; return 0 ;;
    *) echo "cannot compare $1 with remote main (HTTP ${code:-none})" >&2; return 1 ;;
  esac
  case "$rel" in ahead|identical) printf '%s\n' yes ;; *) printf '%s\n' no ;; esac
}
remote_branch() {
  local resp code
  set +e
  resp=$(gh api "repos/libra-tools/libra/git/ref/heads/$1" -i 2>/dev/null)
  set -e
  code=$(printf '%s\n' "$resp" | awk 'NR == 1 { print $2 }')
  case "$code" in
    200) printf '%s\n' "$resp" | sed -n '/^{/,$p' | jq -er '.object.sha' ;;
    404) printf '%s\n' none ;;
    *) echo "cannot read remote branch $1 (HTTP ${code:-none})" >&2; return 1 ;;
  esac
}
case "$CARD" in
  I1055-01) site_cmds='rev-parse cat-file'; site_sentence='Colons inside a ^{/pattern} search are not path separators.' ;;
  I1055-02) site_cmds='show'; site_sentence='In show, colons inside a ^{/pattern} search are not path separators, and a :/pattern search names a commit, so format.pretty applies as in Git.' ;;
  I1055-03) site_cmds='ls-tree'; site_sentence='In ls-tree, colons inside a ^{/pattern} search are not path separators, and a ^{/pattern} search that matches no commit fails instead of resolving to a different commit.' ;;
  *) site_cmds=; site_sentence= ;;
esac
full="$allowed Cargo.toml Cargo.lock install.sh install.ps1 $plan $status_file"
check_c=no
case "${PHASE:?export PHASE=start, accept, c, amend, remote, terminal, blocked or abandon}" in
  c)
    if [ "$c_phase" != yes ]; then echo "$CARD has no C-group commit" >&2; exit 1; fi
    stage="$full"; worktree_ok="$full"; check_c=yes ;;
  amend)
    if [ "$c_phase" != yes ]; then echo "$CARD has no C-group commit" >&2; exit 1; fi
    stage="$plan"; worktree_ok="$plan"; compat_lines=; row_file=; check_c=yes ;;
  accept)
    if [ "$c_phase" != no ]; then echo "$CARD records acceptance with its C-group commit, not PHASE=accept" >&2; exit 1; fi
    stage="$plan $status_file"; worktree_ok="$stage"; compat_lines=; row_file= ;;
  start|remote|terminal) stage="$plan $status_file"; worktree_ok="$stage"; compat_lines=; row_file= ;;
  blocked) stage="$plan $status_file"; worktree_ok="$full"; compat_lines=; row_file= ;;
  abandon)
    if [ -n "$(state_get PUSHED_SHA)" ]; then echo "$CARD has a pushed C-group commit and cannot be abandoned; repair or roll forward instead" >&2; exit 1; fi
    libra fetch origin
    base_version=$(libra cat-file -p HEAD:Cargo.toml | awk -F'"' '/^version = "/ && !seen { print $2; seen = 1 }')
    remote_version=$(libra cat-file -p origin/main:Cargo.toml | awk -F'"' '/^version = "/ && !seen { print $2; seen = 1 }')
    test -n "$base_version"
    if [ "$remote_version" != "$base_version" ]; then echo "origin/main is at $remote_version, not the pre-bump $base_version: a bump may already be on main; do not abandon" >&2; exit 1; fi
    recorded_c=$(state_get C_COMMIT)
    if [ -n "$recorded_c" ] && [ "$(on_remote_main "$recorded_c")" != no ]; then echo "C_COMMIT $recorded_c is on remote main (or the check failed); do not abandon" >&2; exit 1; fi
    next_version=$(printf '%s\n' "$base_version" | awk -F. '{ printf "%d.%d.%d\n", $1, $2, $3 + 1 }')
    if [ "$(remote_branch "release/v$next_version")" != none ]; then echo "release/v$next_version exists on GitHub; do not abandon" >&2; exit 1; fi
    set +e
    tag_code=$(gh api "repos/libra-tools/libra/git/ref/tags/v$next_version" -i 2>/dev/null | awk 'NR == 1 { print $2 }')
    set -e
    if [ "$tag_code" != 404 ]; then echo "tag v$next_version exists or could not be checked (HTTP ${tag_code:-none}); do not abandon" >&2; exit 1; fi
    stage="$plan $status_file"; worktree_ok="$stage"; compat_lines=; row_file= ;;
  *) echo "unknown PHASE=$PHASE" >&2; exit 1 ;;
esac
staged_paths() {
  libra status --porcelain --untracked-files=all | awk 'length($0) > 3 { x = substr($0, 1, 1); if (x != " " && x != "?") print substr($0, 4) }'
}
before=$(staged_paths)
test -z "$before"
for path in $(libra status --porcelain --untracked-files=all | awk 'length($0) > 3 { print substr($0, 4) }'); do
  case " $worktree_ok " in
    *" $path "*) ;;
    *) echo "change outside the allowed set for PHASE=$PHASE: $path" >&2; exit 1 ;;
  esac
done
if [ "$check_c" = yes ]; then
  tree_file="$state_dir/1055-$CARD.tree"
  marker="$state_dir/1055-$CARD.cgates"
  test -s "$tree_file"
  test -s "$marker"
  printf 'HEAD %s\n' "$(libra rev-parse HEAD)" > "$tree_file.now"
  for path in $(libra status --porcelain --untracked-files=all | awk 'length($0) > 3 { print substr($0, 4) }' | sort); do
    if [ "$path" = "$plan" ]; then continue; fi
    if [ -e "$path" ]; then printf '%s %s\n' "$(shasum -a 256 "$path" | cut -d' ' -f1)" "$path"; else printf 'deleted %s\n' "$path"; fi
  done >> "$tree_file.now"
  if ! cmp -s "$tree_file" "$tree_file.now"; then
    carry="$state_dir/1055-$CARD.carry"
    if [ "$PHASE" != amend ] || [ ! -s "$carry" ]; then echo "worktree changed since the C-group snapshot" >&2; exit 1; fi
    carry_get() { awk -v k="$1" 'index($0, k "=") == 1 { v = substr($0, length(k) + 2) } END { print v }' "$carry"; }
    test "$(carry_get TO)" = "$(libra rev-parse HEAD)"
    test "$(carry_get CGATES)" = "$(shasum -a 256 "$marker" | cut -d' ' -f1)"
    orig_c=$(carry_get ORIG_C)
    orig_base=$(carry_get ORIG_BASE)
    test "$(head -n1 "$tree_file")" = "HEAD $orig_base"
    test "$(libra rev-parse "$orig_c^")" = "$orig_base"
    status_delta_ok() {
      local delta hits
      delta=$(libra diff -U0 "$1" "$2" -- docs/development/plan/plan-status.md | awk '/^(\+\+\+|---) (a|b)\// { next } /^[-+]/ { print }') || return 1
      hits=$(printf '%s\n' "$delta" | grep -cF -e '[`plan-20260924.md`]' -e 'DEP-ACF-MIRROR' -e 'DEP-ACF-CAP' || true)
      [ "$hits" = 0 ]
    }
    for path in $(libra diff --name-only "$orig_base" "$(libra rev-parse HEAD^)") $(libra diff --name-only "$orig_c" HEAD); do
      case "$path" in
        docs/development/plan/plan-status.md) ;;
        docs/development/plan/*)
          set +e
          rg -l -F -- "${path##*/}" tests src build.rs .github >/dev/null
          rg_rc=$?
          set -e
          if [ "$rg_rc" != 1 ]; then echo "not carryable: $path is referenced by tests or the build (rg exit $rg_rc)" >&2; exit 1; fi ;;
        *) echo "not carryable: $path" >&2; exit 1 ;;
      esac
    done
    if ! status_delta_ok "$orig_base" "$(libra rev-parse HEAD^)" || ! status_delta_ok "$orig_c" "$(libra rev-parse HEAD)"; then
      echo "not carryable: a plan-status.md line read by tests changed (or the diff failed)" >&2
      exit 1
    fi
  fi
  fresh="$state_dir/1055-$CARD.gates.check.sh"
  fresh_c="$state_dir/1055-$CARD.c.check.sh"
  python3 -I - "$plan" "$CARD" "$fresh" "$fresh_c" <<'EXTRACT'
import json, re, sys
plan, card, out = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2], sys.argv[3]
cfg = json.loads(r"""{"gates": {"I1055-01": [["all", "**bump 前窗口检查（"], ["all", "**版本面内容检查（"], ["all", "**精确句预检（"], ["all", "先执行前置块，再执行 I1055-01 Verification 中的两门"], ["card", "**判据规范（非计数正文）：**"], ["all", "每卡在提交前于最终树执行"]], "I1055-02": [["all", "**bump 前窗口检查（"], ["all", "**版本面内容检查（"], ["all", "**精确句预检（"], ["card", "**Verification:** 下列八个"], ["all", "每卡在提交前于最终树执行"]], "I1055-03": [["all", "**bump 前窗口检查（"], ["all", "**版本面内容检查（"], ["all", "**精确句预检（"], ["card", "**Verification:** 下列四个"], ["all", "每卡在提交前于最终树执行"]]}, "c": ["all", "C 组先运行"], "no_c": []}""")
raw = open(plan, encoding='utf-8').read()
chars = list(raw)
F = chr(96) * 3

def mask(lo, hi):
    for k in range(lo, hi):
        if chars[k] != '\n':
            chars[k] = ' '

for m in re.finditer('(?ms)^' + F + r'[^\n]*\n.*?^' + F + r'[ \t]*$', raw):
    mask(m.start(), m.end())
for m in re.finditer(r'(?m)^## (?:评审结论与修订记录|实施证据)[^\n]*$', raw):
    n = re.compile(r'(?m)^## ').search(raw, m.end())
    mask(m.start(), n.start() if n else len(raw))
prose = ''.join(chars)
heading = re.compile(r'(?m)^#{2,4} Task ')

def die(msg):
    print('C-group extract: ' + msg, file=sys.stderr)
    sys.exit(1)

def span(scope):
    if scope == 'all':
        return 0, len(prose)
    m = re.search(r'(?m)^#{2,4} Task ' + re.escape(card) + r'(?![\w-])', prose)
    if not m:
        die('no Task heading for ' + card)
    n = heading.search(prose, m.end())
    return m.start(), (n.start() if n else len(prose))

def block(scope, anchor):
    lo, hi = span(scope)
    hits = [m.start() for m in re.finditer(re.escape(anchor), prose[lo:hi])]
    if len(hits) != 1:
        die(f'anchor {anchor!r} matched {len(hits)} times in the prose of scope {scope}')
    pos = lo + hits[0]
    m = re.compile('(?m)^' + F + r'bash\n').search(raw, pos)
    if not m:
        die(f'no bash block after {anchor!r}')
    if re.search(r'(?m)^#{1,6} ', prose[pos:m.start()]):
        die(f'a heading separates {anchor!r} from its bash block')
    end = raw.find('\n' + F + '\n', m.end() - 1)
    if end < 0:
        die(f'unterminated bash block after {anchor!r}')
    return raw[m.end():end + 1]

if card not in cfg['gates']:
    die('unknown CARD=' + card)
c_out = sys.argv[4]
if c_out != '-' and card in cfg['no_c']:
    die(card + ' has no C-group commit; the C-group runner does not apply')
head = ['set -eo pipefail', 'cd /Volumes/Data/gitmono/libra', 'export CARD=' + card]
parts = head + ['export VSRC=worktree']
for i, (scope, anchor) in enumerate(cfg['gates'][card], 1):
    parts += ['# --- gate block %d: %s' % (i, anchor), 'set +u', block(scope, anchor)]
parts.append('echo "gate blocks passed for $CARD"')
open(out, 'w', encoding='utf-8').write('\n'.join(parts) + '\n')
if c_out != '-':
    c_parts = head + ['# --- C-group script: %s' % cfg['c'][1], block(cfg['c'][0], cfg['c'][1]), 'echo "C-group script passed for $CARD"']
    open(c_out, 'w', encoding='utf-8').write('\n'.join(c_parts) + '\n')
if len(sys.argv) > 5:
    open(sys.argv[5], 'w', encoding='utf-8').write(block('all', '**工作区独占检查（'))

EXTRACT
  want=$(printf 'TREE=%s\nGATES=%s\nC=%s' "$(shasum -a 256 "$tree_file" | cut -d' ' -f1)" "$(shasum -a 256 "$fresh" | cut -d' ' -f1)" "$(shasum -a 256 "$fresh_c" | cut -d' ' -f1)")
  if [ "$(cat "$marker")" != "$want" ]; then echo "no C-group pass marker for this tree and these gate blocks; rerun the C-group runner" >&2; exit 1; fi
fi
if [ "$PHASE" = c ]; then
  review_digest() {
    libra status --porcelain --untracked-files=all | awk 'length($0) > 3 { print substr($0, 4) }' | sort | while IFS= read -r path; do
      case "$path" in "$plan"|"$status_file"|Cargo.toml|Cargo.lock|install.sh|install.ps1) continue ;; esac
      if [ -e "$path" ]; then printf '%s %s\n' "$(shasum -a 256 "$path" | cut -d' ' -f1)" "$path"; else printf 'deleted %s\n' "$path"; fi
    done | shasum -a 256 | cut -d' ' -f1
  }
  er05_line() {
    local hits
    hits=$(grep -E "^$CARD:$1=$2\$" "$plan" || true)
    if [ "$(printf '%s\n' "$hits" | grep -c .)" != 1 ]; then echo "need exactly one line $CARD:$1=<$2> in the implementation evidence" >&2; return 1; fi
    printf '%s\n' "${hits#*=}"
  }
  er05_line ER05_SESSION '[0-9A-Za-z._:-]{8,}' >/dev/null
  er05_line ER05_OUTPUT_SHA256 '[0-9a-f]{64}' >/dev/null
  er05_line ER05_VERDICT 'PASS' >/dev/null
  reviewed=$(er05_line ER05_REVIEWED '[0-9a-f]{64}')
  current=$(review_digest)
  if [ "$reviewed" != "$current" ]; then echo "product files changed after the ER-05 review (reviewed $reviewed, now $current); review again and replace the four ER-05 lines" >&2; exit 1; fi
fi
own='| [`issues/1055.md`](issues/1055.md) |'
if [ "$(grep -cF -- "$own" "$status_file")" != 1 ]; then echo "plan-status.md must contain exactly one row for this plan in the worktree" >&2; exit 1; fi
if [ "$(libra cat-file -p "HEAD:$status_file" | grep -cF -- "$own")" != 1 ]; then echo "plan-status.md must contain exactly one row for this plan at HEAD" >&2; exit 1; fi
for path in $stage; do
  if [ -f "$path" ] && grep -nE '^(<<<<<<< |\|\|\|\|\|\|\| |>>>>>>> |=======$)' "$path" >&2; then echo "conflict marker lines in $path" >&2; exit 1; fi
done
status_diff=$(libra diff -U0 "$status_file")
foreign=$(printf '%s\n' "$status_diff" | awk -v p="$own" '
  /^@@/ { in_hunk = 1; next }
  !in_hunk { next }
  /^[-+]/ { body = substr($0, 2); if (index(body, p) != 1) print "foreign line: " $0 }
')
test -z "$foreign"
if [ -n "$compat_lines" ]; then
  check_compat_rows COMPATIBILITY.md
  libra cat-file -p HEAD:COMPATIBILITY.md > "$state_dir/1055-$CARD.compat-head"
  check_compat_rows "$state_dir/1055-$CARD.compat-head"
  compat_diff=$(libra diff -U0 COMPATIBILITY.md)
  if [ -z "$compat_diff" ]; then
    if [ -z "$(state_get PUSHED_SHA)" ]; then echo "the first C-group commit of $CARD must change COMPATIBILITY.md line(s) $compat_lines" >&2; exit 1; fi
  else
    bad=$(printf '%s\n' "$compat_diff" | awk -v ok=" $compat_lines " '
      /^@@/ {
        split($2, o, ","); split($3, n, ",")
        start = substr(o[1], 2); oc = (o[2] == "" ? 1 : o[2]); nc = (n[2] == "" ? 1 : n[2])
        if (oc != 1 || nc != 1 || substr(n[1], 2) != start || index(ok, " " start " ") == 0) print "unexpected hunk: " $0
      }')
    if [ -n "$bad" ]; then printf '%s\n' "$bad" >&2; exit 1; fi
  fi
fi
if [ -n "$row_file" ]; then
  row_diff=$(libra diff -U0 "$row_file")
  if [ -n "$row_diff" ]; then
    row_bad=$(printf '%s\n' "$row_diff" | awk -v p="$row_prefix" '
      /^@@/ { in_hunk = 1; next }
      !in_hunk { next }
      /^-/ { del++; if (index(substr($0, 2), p) != 1) print "foreign line: " $0 }
      /^\+/ { add++; if (index(substr($0, 2), p) != 1) print "foreign line: " $0 }
      END { if (add != del) print "row count changed: +" add " -" del }')
    test -z "$row_bad"
  fi
fi
python3 -I - "$plan" "$CARD" "$PHASE" "$state_dir/1055-$CARD.env" <<'TRANSITION'
import json, re, subprocess, sys
plan, card, phase, state_path = sys.argv[1:5]
cfg = json.loads(r"""{"linked": {}, "site": true}""")

def die(msg):
    print('card-state check: ' + msg, file=sys.stderr)
    sys.exit(1)

def norm(value):
    value = re.sub('（[^）]*）|[(][^)]*[)]', '', value.replace(chr(96), ''))
    life, _, acc = value.partition('/')
    life = re.split('[（(]', (life.split() or [''])[0])[0]
    acc = re.split('[（(]', (acc.split() or [''])[0])[0].strip('。；;,，')
    return (life, acc if acc not in ('', '空') else '空')

def fields(text):
    out = {}
    for body in re.split(r'(?m)^#{2,4} Task ', text)[1:]:
        cid = re.split(r'[:：\s]', body, maxsplit=1)[0]
        m = re.search(r'(?m)^\*\*Lifecycle / Acceptance:\*\*[ \t]*(.*)$', body)
        out[cid] = norm(m.group(1)) if m else None
    return out

head = subprocess.run(['libra', 'cat-file', '-p', 'HEAD:' + plan], capture_output=True, text=True)
if head.returncode:
    die('cannot read HEAD:' + plan)
text = open(plan, encoding='utf-8').read()
old, new = fields(head.stdout), fields(text)
if set(old) != set(new):
    die('the set of Task cards differs between HEAD and the worktree')
if new.get(card) is None:
    die('no Lifecycle / Acceptance field for ' + card)
P, LA, RP = ('in-progress', '空'), ('in-progress', 'locally-accepted'), ('in-progress', 'remote-pending')
ACC = ('空', 'locally-accepted', 'remote-pending')
ALLOWED = {
    'start': {(('pending', '空'), P)},
    'accept': {(P, LA)},
    'c': {(P, LA), (LA, LA), (RP, RP)},
    'amend': {(LA, LA), (RP, RP)},
    'remote': {(LA, RP), (RP, RP)},
    'terminal': {(RP, ('done', 'complete'))},
    'blocked': {(('in-progress', a), ('blocked', a)) for a in ACC} | {(('blocked', a), ('in-progress', a)) for a in ACC},
    'abandon': {((life, a), ('pending', '空')) for life in ('in-progress', 'blocked') for a in ('空', 'locally-accepted')},
}
move = (old[card], new[card])
if phase == 'blocked' and old[card][1] != new[card][1]:
    die(f'{card}: PHASE=blocked keeps the committed Acceptance {old[card][1]!r}; put it back in the worktree (an uncommitted locally-accepted is rewritten after unblocking), then rerun')
if move not in ALLOWED[phase]:
    die(f'{card}: {move[0]} -> {move[1]} is not allowed for PHASE={phase}')
linked = cfg['linked'].get(card, [])
for cid in new:
    if cid == card:
        continue
    if cid in linked and phase in ('remote', 'terminal'):
        if (old[cid], new[cid]) != move:
            die(f'{cid} must move with {card}: {old[cid]} -> {new[cid]}')
    elif old[cid] != new[cid]:
        die(f'{cid} changed ({old[cid]} -> {new[cid]}) in a PHASE={phase} commit for {card}')
state = {}
try:
    with open(state_path, encoding='utf-8') as fh:
        for line in fh:
            key, sep, value = line.rstrip('\n').partition('=')
            if sep:
                state[key] = value
except FileNotFoundError:
    pass
keys = {'remote': ['C_COMMIT', 'PUSHED_SHA', 'VERSION'],
        'terminal': ['CI_BASE_RUN', 'CI_CODEQL_RUN', 'TAG', 'RELEASE_RUN', 'CANDIDATE_DELETED', 'ROLLBACK_VERIFIED'] + (['SITE_SHA', 'SITE_VERIFIED', 'SITE_REVIEWED'] if cfg['site'] else [])}.get(phase, [])
for key in keys:
    value = state.get(key, '')
    if not value:
        die(f'the state file has no {key} for {card}')
    if not re.search('(?m)^' + re.escape(f'{card}:{key}={value}') + '$', text):
        die(f'the implementation evidence needs the line {card}:{key}={value}')
if phase == 'terminal' and state.get('CANDIDATE_DELETED') != 'yes':
    die('CANDIDATE_DELETED is not yes')
if phase == 'terminal' and cfg['site']:
    for key, pat in (('SITE_REVIEW_SESSION', '[0-9A-Za-z._:-]{8,}'), ('SITE_REVIEW_OUTPUT_SHA256', '[0-9a-f]{64}'), ('SITE_REVIEW_VERDICT', 'PASS'), ('SITE_REVIEWED', '[0-9a-f]{64}')):
        if len(re.findall('(?m)^' + re.escape(card + ':' + key + '=') + pat + '$', text)) != 1:
            die(f'the implementation evidence needs exactly one line {card}:{key}=<{pat}>')

TRANSITION
case "$PHASE" in
  remote)
    pushed=$(state_get PUSHED_SHA)
    test "$(on_remote_main "$pushed")" = yes
    test "$(remote_branch "release/v$(state_get VERSION)")" = "$pushed" ;;
  terminal)
    pushed=$(state_get PUSHED_SHA)
    version=$(state_get VERSION)
    test -n "$pushed"
    test -n "$version"
    test "$(state_get TAG)" = "v$version"
    ref=$(gh api "repos/libra-tools/libra/git/ref/tags/v$version" --jq '"\(.object.type) \(.object.sha)"')
    read -r ttype tobj <<< "$ref"
    if [ "$ttype" = tag ]; then tobj=$(gh api "repos/libra-tools/libra/git/tags/$tobj" --jq .object.sha); fi
    test "$tobj" = "$pushed"
    for key in BASE CODEQL; do
      run=$(state_get "CI_${key}_RUN")
      test -n "$run"
      view=$(gh run view "$run" --repo libra-tools/libra --json conclusion,headSha,headBranch,event --jq '"\(.conclusion) \(.headSha) \(.headBranch) \(.event)"')
      test "$view" = "success $pushed release/v$version push"
    done
    release_run=$(state_get RELEASE_RUN)
    test -n "$release_run"
    rview=$(gh run view "$release_run" --repo libra-tools/libra --json conclusion,headSha,headBranch --jq '"\(.conclusion) \(.headSha) \(.headBranch)"')
    test "$rview" = "success $pushed v$version"
    gh run view "$release_run" --repo libra-tools/libra --json jobs |
      jq -e '.jobs as $jobs
        | ["build-and-upload","upload-install-scripts","update-homebrew-tap","verify-homebrew-formula","request-stable-manifest"]
        | all(.[]; . as $j | [$jobs[] | select(.name | startswith($j))] | (length > 0) and all(.[]; .conclusion == "success"))' >/dev/null
    test "$(remote_branch "release/v$version")" = none
    if [ -n "$site_cmds" ]; then
      site_sha=$(state_get SITE_SHA)
      test -n "$site_sha"
      test -n "$(state_get SITE_VERIFIED)"
      for command in $site_cmds; do
        for locale in en zh-CN; do
          body=$(curl -fsS --max-time 60 "https://libra.tools/${locale}/docs/commands/${command}")
          test "$(printf '%s\n' "$body" | grep -cF "$site_sentence")" -ge 1
        done
      done
      (
        cd /Volumes/Sea/libra-backend
        test "$(pwd -P)" = /Volumes/Sea/libra-backend
        libra fetch origin
        test "$(libra rev-list origin/cf | grep -cxF "$site_sha")" = 1
      )
    fi ;;
  accept)
    accept_gates="$state_dir/1055-$CARD.accept.sh"
    python3 -I - "$plan" "$CARD" "$accept_gates" - <<'EXTRACT'
import json, re, sys
plan, card, out = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2], sys.argv[3]
cfg = json.loads(r"""{"gates": {"I1055-01": [["all", "**bump 前窗口检查（"], ["all", "**版本面内容检查（"], ["all", "**精确句预检（"], ["all", "先执行前置块，再执行 I1055-01 Verification 中的两门"], ["card", "**判据规范（非计数正文）：**"], ["all", "每卡在提交前于最终树执行"]], "I1055-02": [["all", "**bump 前窗口检查（"], ["all", "**版本面内容检查（"], ["all", "**精确句预检（"], ["card", "**Verification:** 下列八个"], ["all", "每卡在提交前于最终树执行"]], "I1055-03": [["all", "**bump 前窗口检查（"], ["all", "**版本面内容检查（"], ["all", "**精确句预检（"], ["card", "**Verification:** 下列四个"], ["all", "每卡在提交前于最终树执行"]]}, "c": ["all", "C 组先运行"], "no_c": []}""")
raw = open(plan, encoding='utf-8').read()
chars = list(raw)
F = chr(96) * 3

def mask(lo, hi):
    for k in range(lo, hi):
        if chars[k] != '\n':
            chars[k] = ' '

for m in re.finditer('(?ms)^' + F + r'[^\n]*\n.*?^' + F + r'[ \t]*$', raw):
    mask(m.start(), m.end())
for m in re.finditer(r'(?m)^## (?:评审结论与修订记录|实施证据)[^\n]*$', raw):
    n = re.compile(r'(?m)^## ').search(raw, m.end())
    mask(m.start(), n.start() if n else len(raw))
prose = ''.join(chars)
heading = re.compile(r'(?m)^#{2,4} Task ')

def die(msg):
    print('C-group extract: ' + msg, file=sys.stderr)
    sys.exit(1)

def span(scope):
    if scope == 'all':
        return 0, len(prose)
    m = re.search(r'(?m)^#{2,4} Task ' + re.escape(card) + r'(?![\w-])', prose)
    if not m:
        die('no Task heading for ' + card)
    n = heading.search(prose, m.end())
    return m.start(), (n.start() if n else len(prose))

def block(scope, anchor):
    lo, hi = span(scope)
    hits = [m.start() for m in re.finditer(re.escape(anchor), prose[lo:hi])]
    if len(hits) != 1:
        die(f'anchor {anchor!r} matched {len(hits)} times in the prose of scope {scope}')
    pos = lo + hits[0]
    m = re.compile('(?m)^' + F + r'bash\n').search(raw, pos)
    if not m:
        die(f'no bash block after {anchor!r}')
    if re.search(r'(?m)^#{1,6} ', prose[pos:m.start()]):
        die(f'a heading separates {anchor!r} from its bash block')
    end = raw.find('\n' + F + '\n', m.end() - 1)
    if end < 0:
        die(f'unterminated bash block after {anchor!r}')
    return raw[m.end():end + 1]

if card not in cfg['gates']:
    die('unknown CARD=' + card)
c_out = sys.argv[4]
if c_out != '-' and card in cfg['no_c']:
    die(card + ' has no C-group commit; the C-group runner does not apply')
head = ['set -eo pipefail', 'cd /Volumes/Data/gitmono/libra', 'export CARD=' + card]
parts = head + ['export VSRC=worktree']
for i, (scope, anchor) in enumerate(cfg['gates'][card], 1):
    parts += ['# --- gate block %d: %s' % (i, anchor), 'set +u', block(scope, anchor)]
parts.append('echo "gate blocks passed for $CARD"')
open(out, 'w', encoding='utf-8').write('\n'.join(parts) + '\n')
if c_out != '-':
    c_parts = head + ['# --- C-group script: %s' % cfg['c'][1], block(cfg['c'][0], cfg['c'][1]), 'echo "C-group script passed for $CARD"']
    open(c_out, 'w', encoding='utf-8').write('\n'.join(c_parts) + '\n')
if len(sys.argv) > 5:
    open(sys.argv[5], 'w', encoding='utf-8').write(block('all', '**工作区独占检查（'))

EXTRACT
    bash "$accept_gates" ;;
esac
for path in $stage; do
  if [ -e "$path" ]; then libra add "$path"; fi
done
staged=$(staged_paths)
test -n "$staged"
for path in $staged; do
  case " $stage " in
    *" $path "*) ;;
    *) echo "staged path outside the PHASE=$PHASE set: $path" >&2; exit 1 ;;
  esac
done
```

**提交后范围检查（`bash` 运行，`PHASE` 取 `start`、`accept`、`c`、`remote`、`terminal`、`blocked` 或 `abandon`；amend 之后用 `c`）：** 刚生成的提交只能改该 `PHASE` 允许集合内的路径，且只有一个父提交。

```bash
set -eo pipefail
cd /Volumes/Data/gitmono/libra
plan=docs/development/plan/issues/1055.md
status_file=docs/development/plan/plan-status.md
case "${CARD:?export CARD first}" in
  I1055-01) allowed='src/utils/util.rs tests/command/cat_file_test.rs tests/command/rev_parse_test.rs docs/commands/rev-parse.md docs/commands/zh-CN/rev-parse.md docs/development/commands/rev-parse.md docs/commands/cat-file.md docs/commands/zh-CN/cat-file.md docs/development/commands/cat-file.md COMPATIBILITY.md'; compat_lines='217 265'; c_phase=yes; row_file=''; row_prefix='' ;;
  I1055-02) allowed='src/command/show.rs tests/command/show_test.rs docs/commands/show.md docs/commands/zh-CN/show.md docs/development/commands/show.md COMPATIBILITY.md'; compat_lines='199'; c_phase=yes; row_file=''; row_prefix='' ;;
  I1055-03) allowed='src/command/ls_tree.rs tests/command/ls_tree_test.rs docs/commands/ls-tree.md docs/commands/zh-CN/ls-tree.md docs/development/commands/ls-tree.md COMPATIBILITY.md'; compat_lines='203'; c_phase=yes; row_file=''; row_prefix='' ;;
  *) echo "unknown CARD=$CARD" >&2; exit 1 ;;
esac
case "${PHASE:?export PHASE=start, accept, c, remote, terminal, blocked or abandon}" in
  c)
    if [ "$c_phase" != yes ]; then echo "$CARD has no C-group commit" >&2; exit 1; fi
    allowed="$allowed Cargo.toml Cargo.lock install.sh install.ps1 $plan $status_file" ;;
  accept)
    if [ "$c_phase" != no ]; then echo "$CARD records acceptance with its C-group commit, not PHASE=accept" >&2; exit 1; fi
    allowed="$plan $status_file" ;;
  start|remote|terminal|blocked|abandon) allowed="$plan $status_file" ;;
  *) echo "unknown PHASE=$PHASE" >&2; exit 1 ;;
esac
test "$(libra cat-file -p HEAD | grep -c '^parent ')" = 1
changed=$(libra diff --name-only HEAD^ HEAD)
test -n "$changed"
for path in $changed; do
  case " $allowed " in
    *" $path "*) ;;
    *) echo "committed path outside write set: $path" >&2; exit 1 ;;
  esac
done
test "$(libra cat-file -p "HEAD:$status_file" | grep -cF -- '| [`issues/1055.md`](issues/1055.md) |')" = 1
```

**计划目录推送脚本（`bash` 运行，`PHASE` 取 `start`、`accept`、`remote`、`terminal`、`blocked` 或 `abandon`）：** 推送前核对：暂存区为空；远端 `main` 上没有其它提交的在途 run（只放行提交说明以 `(#1055)` 结尾的本计划提交的 run，`gh` 查询或读取提交失败同样停止；推送会让 `cancel-superseded-main` 取消它们，所以有就停止，等它们结束后原样重跑）；工作区干净（`PHASE=blocked` 时允许本卡允许集合内未提交的改动）；当前分支是 `main` 且指向被检查的提交；该提交只有一个父提交，带 `gpgsig` 与 `Signed-off-by`（直接读 `libra cat-file -p` 的提交对象：`libra log -1 --only-trailers --trailer Signed-off-by` 会返回最近一个带该 trailer 的祖先提交，2026-11-07 已实测，不能用来证明本提交）；它只改本文件与 `plan-status.md`。`PHASE=start` 时，在 fetch 并确认父提交就是 `origin/main` 之后、推送之前，从本文件抽出开工门原样重跑。被检查提交的父提交必须已在远端 `main` 上：父提交是尚未推送的提交（例如 C 组提交）时脚本直接拒绝，不进入 rebase，因为推送会把那个提交一并带上 `main`。推送通过一个临时本地分支 `plan-push-1055-<CARD>` 进行，该分支在检查之后被强制指向被检查的 SHA，所以推上远端 `main` 的对象就是它，即使本地 `main` 在此期间被移动。推送结果按远端事实判定：fetch 后 `origin/main` 等于该 SHA，或 GitHub compare 显示该 SHA 已在远端 `main` 上，就算推送成功（客户端报错但推送已落地、或他人紧接着推送，都不误判，重跑本脚本也直接确认成功）；`origin/main` 仍是父提交，说明是认证、网络或服务端错误，按 ER-09 直接非零退出，不 rebase、不 amend、不计数；`origin/main` 已前进到别处时进入有界恢复：`libra rebase origin/main`（`PHASE=blocked` 且工作区有本卡未提交改动时加 `--autostash`，rebase 前后由 libra 暂存并恢复这些改动；失败时只有确实存在进行中的 rebase 才 `--abort`，并区分「冲突」与「拒绝开始」），成功后才把计数加一，`libra commit --amend -S --no-edit` 重签（不加 `-s`，原 `Signed-off-by` 已随重放保留），以退出码 3 停止。执行者随后重跑提交后范围检查（`PHASE=start` 时还要重跑 `WHEN=start` 的外部前置检查；开工门由本脚本自己重跑），再运行本脚本。计数文件按计划、卡与 `PHASE` 命名，第 4 次遇到上游前进时脚本非零退出：开工登记未推送时本卡保持 `pending`；其它登记未推送时本卡保持当前状态；都向用户报告，用户决定继续时由执行者删除该计数文件。不用 `libra pull` 合并上游，因为合并提交有两个父提交，推送检查会拒绝。

```bash
set -eo pipefail
cd /Volumes/Data/gitmono/libra
plan=docs/development/plan/issues/1055.md
status_file=docs/development/plan/plan-status.md
case "${CARD:?export CARD first}" in
  I1055-01) allowed='src/utils/util.rs tests/command/cat_file_test.rs tests/command/rev_parse_test.rs docs/commands/rev-parse.md docs/commands/zh-CN/rev-parse.md docs/development/commands/rev-parse.md docs/commands/cat-file.md docs/commands/zh-CN/cat-file.md docs/development/commands/cat-file.md COMPATIBILITY.md'; compat_lines='217 265'; c_phase=yes; row_file=''; row_prefix='' ;;
  I1055-02) allowed='src/command/show.rs tests/command/show_test.rs docs/commands/show.md docs/commands/zh-CN/show.md docs/development/commands/show.md COMPATIBILITY.md'; compat_lines='199'; c_phase=yes; row_file=''; row_prefix='' ;;
  I1055-03) allowed='src/command/ls_tree.rs tests/command/ls_tree_test.rs docs/commands/ls-tree.md docs/commands/zh-CN/ls-tree.md docs/development/commands/ls-tree.md COMPATIBILITY.md'; compat_lines='203'; c_phase=yes; row_file=''; row_prefix='' ;;
  *) echo "unknown CARD=$CARD" >&2; exit 1 ;;
esac
on_remote_main() {
  local resp code rel
  set +e
  resp=$(gh api "repos/libra-tools/libra/compare/$1...main" -i 2>/dev/null)
  set -e
  code=$(printf '%s\n' "$resp" | awk 'NR == 1 { print $2 }')
  case "$code" in
    200) rel=$(printf '%s\n' "$resp" | sed -n '/^{/,$p' | jq -er .status) || return 1 ;;
    404) printf '%s\n' no; return 0 ;;
    *) echo "cannot compare $1 with remote main (HTTP ${code:-none})" >&2; return 1 ;;
  esac
  case "$rel" in ahead|identical) printf '%s\n' yes ;; *) printf '%s\n' no ;; esac
}
case "${PHASE:?export PHASE=start, accept, remote, terminal, blocked or abandon}" in
  start|accept|remote|terminal|abandon) worktree_ok= ;;
  blocked) worktree_ok="$allowed Cargo.toml Cargo.lock install.sh install.ps1" ;;
  *) echo "unknown PHASE=$PHASE" >&2; exit 1 ;;
esac
counter="/Volumes/Data/tmp/libra-plan-push-1055-$CARD-$PHASE.count"
porcelain=$(libra status --porcelain --untracked-files=all)
staged=$(printf '%s\n' "$porcelain" | awk 'length($0) > 3 { x = substr($0, 1, 1); if (x != " " && x != "?") print substr($0, 4) }')
test -z "$staged"
for path in $(printf '%s\n' "$porcelain" | awk 'length($0) > 3 { print substr($0, 4) }'); do
  case " $worktree_ok " in
    *" $path "*) ;;
    *) echo "uncommitted change not allowed for PHASE=$PHASE: $path" >&2; exit 1 ;;
  esac
done
test "$(libra branch --show-current)" = main
SHA=$(libra rev-parse HEAD)
test "$(libra rev-parse main)" = "$SHA"
obj=$(libra cat-file -p "$SHA")
test "$(printf '%s\n' "$obj" | grep -c '^parent ')" = 1
test "$(printf '%s\n' "$obj" | grep -c '^gpgsig ')" = 1
test "$(printf '%s\n' "$obj" | grep -c '^Signed-off-by: ')" -ge 1
changed=$(libra diff --name-only "$SHA^" "$SHA")
test -n "$changed"
test -z "$(printf '%s\n' "$changed" | grep -vxF -e "$plan" -e "$status_file" || true)"
parent=$(libra rev-parse "$SHA^")
libra fetch origin
remote_main=$(libra rev-parse origin/main)
test -n "$remote_main"
if [ "$remote_main" != "$SHA" ] && [ "$parent" = "$remote_main" ]; then
  if [ "$PHASE" = start ]; then
    gate_script=$(mktemp /Volumes/Data/tmp/start-gate.XXXXXX)
    python3 -I - "$plan" "$gate_script" <<'GATEX'
import re, sys
text = open(sys.argv[1], encoding='utf-8').read()
hits = [m.start() for m in re.finditer(r'(?m)^\*\*开工门（`bash` 运行', text)]
if len(hits) != 1:
    sys.exit('start gate anchor matched %d times' % len(hits))
fence = chr(96) * 3
m = re.compile('(?m)^' + fence + r'bash\n').search(text, hits[0])
end = text.find('\n' + fence + '\n', m.end() - 1)
open(sys.argv[2], 'w', encoding='utf-8').write(text[m.end():end + 1])
GATEX
    bash "$gate_script"
  fi
  runs=$(gh run list --repo libra-tools/libra --branch main --limit 100 --json databaseId,status,workflowName,headSha --jq '.[] | select(.status != "completed") | select(.workflowName != "Cancel superseded main Actions") | "\(.databaseId) \(.headSha)"')
  busy=''
  for run_sha in $(printf '%s\n' "$runs" | awk 'NF == 2 { print $2 }' | sort -u); do
    run_obj=$(libra cat-file -p "$run_sha") || { echo "cannot read commit $run_sha of a run on main; fetch and rerun" >&2; exit 1; }
    run_subject=$(printf '%s\n' "$run_obj" | awk 'body && !seen { print; seen = 1 } /^$/ { body = 1 }')
    case "$run_subject" in *"(#1055)") ;; *) busy="$busy $run_sha" ;; esac
  done
  if [ -n "$busy" ]; then echo "CI of other commits is still running on main ($busy); pushing now would make cancel-superseded-main cancel it; wait until those runs complete, then rerun" >&2; exit 1; fi
  push_branch="plan-push-1055-$CARD"
  libra branch -f "$push_branch" "$SHA"
  set +e
  libra push origin "$push_branch:main"
  push_rc=$?
  set -e
  libra branch -D "$push_branch" || echo "warning: could not delete the local branch $push_branch" >&2
  echo "push exit code $push_rc; checking the remote" >&2
fi
libra fetch origin
latest=$(libra rev-parse origin/main)
test -n "$latest"
landed=yes
if [ "$latest" != "$SHA" ]; then landed=$(on_remote_main "$SHA"); fi
if [ "$landed" = yes ]; then
  rm -f "$counter"
  echo "pushed $SHA"
  exit 0
fi
if [ "$latest" = "$parent" ]; then
  echo "push failed while origin/main did not move: authentication, network or server error; not retrying (ER-09)" >&2
  exit 1
fi
parent_on_main=$(on_remote_main "$parent")
if [ "$parent_on_main" = no ]; then
  echo "the parent $parent is not on remote main (an unpushed commit such as the C-group commit); this script only pushes one plan commit on top of origin/main" >&2
  exit 1
fi
attempts=$(( $(cat "$counter" 2>/dev/null || echo 0) + 1 ))
if [ "$attempts" -gt 3 ]; then echo "origin/main advanced $attempts times; stop and report" >&2; exit 1; fi
rebase_opts=
if [ -n "$worktree_ok" ] && [ -n "$(printf '%s\n' "$porcelain" | awk 'length($0) > 3')" ]; then rebase_opts=--autostash; fi
if ! libra rebase $rebase_opts origin/main; then
  if libra rebase --abort; then
    echo "rebase stopped on a conflict and was aborted: rebase by hand (plan-status.md: take origin/main and replay only this plan's row), re-sign with libra commit --amend -S --no-edit, rerun the staging guard, then rerun this script" >&2
  else
    echo "rebase did not start (see the error above); nothing to abort" >&2
  fi
  exit 1
fi
echo "$attempts" > "$counter"
gpgsig_digest() { libra cat-file -p "$1" | awk '/^gpgsig /{ f = 1; print; next } f && /^ /{ print; next } f { exit }' | shasum -a 256 | cut -d' ' -f1; }
libra commit --amend -S --no-edit
if [ "$(gpgsig_digest HEAD)" = "$(gpgsig_digest "$SHA")" ]; then echo "the replayed commit still carries the old signature; libra rebase copies gpgsig verbatim, so the amend did not re-sign" >&2; exit 1; fi
echo "rebased onto origin/main (attempt $attempts): rerun the post-commit range check (and for PHASE=start the external prerequisite check), then rerun this script" >&2
exit 3
```

### `COMPATIBILITY.md` 约束

I1055-01 只改 `:217`（rev-parse）与 `:265`（cat-file），I1055-02 只改 `:199`（show），I1055-03 只改 `:203`（ls-tree），各在原行描述单元格内、结尾的 `|` 之前追加本卡精确句（I1055-01 为 `Colons inside a ^{/pattern} search are not path separators.`，I1055-03 为 `In ls-tree, colons inside a ^{/pattern} search are not path separators, and a ^{/pattern} search that matches no commit fails instead of resolving to a different commit.`，I1055-02 为 `In show, colons inside a ^{/pattern} search are not path separators, and a :/pattern search names a commit, so format.pretty applies as in Git.`），不换行，也不追加其它规则说明。不改 `:277`，不增删行。暂存守卫按这些行号限制 hunk。

### 文档门

每卡在提交前于最终树执行（`bash` 运行，先 `export CARD=<本卡 ID>`）：

```bash
set -eo pipefail
cd /Volumes/Data/gitmono/libra
case "${CARD:?export CARD first}" in
  I1055-01) lines='217 265'; sentence='Colons inside a ^{/pattern} search are not path separators.' ;;
  I1055-02) lines='199'; sentence='In show, colons inside a ^{/pattern} search are not path separators, and a :/pattern search names a commit, so format.pretty applies as in Git.' ;;
  I1055-03) lines='203'; sentence='In ls-tree, colons inside a ^{/pattern} search are not path separators, and a ^{/pattern} search that matches no commit fails instead of resolving to a different commit.' ;;
  *) echo "unknown CARD=$CARD" >&2; exit 1 ;;
esac
for n in $lines; do
  test "$(awk -v n="$n" 'NR == n' COMPATIBILITY.md | grep -cF "$sentence")" = 1
done
before=$(libra cat-file -p HEAD:COMPATIBILITY.md | wc -l | tr -d ' ')
after=$(wc -l < COMPATIBILITY.md | tr -d ' ')
test "$before" = "$after"
if [ "$CARD" = I1055-03 ]; then
  if rg -n -F '在首个 `:` 处切分' docs/development/commands/ls-tree.md; then
    echo "stale first-colon wording in the ls-tree development doc" >&2; exit 1
  else
    rc=$?; test "$rc" -eq 1
  fi
fi
```

### TC-3285 前置与门

先执行前置块，再执行 I1055-01 Verification 中的两门（`run_1055_tc git`、`run_1055_tc libra`）：

```bash
set -euo pipefail
cd /Volumes/Data/gitmono/libra-testcases
test "$(git rev-parse HEAD)" = b8ad10bd14d95ffc03cd55927a37f31e67d8ff42
test -z "$(git status --porcelain)"
export CARGO_TARGET_DIR=/Volumes/Data/gitmono/libra/target
export GIT_REF_REPO=/Volumes/Data/gitmono/git
export TC_TMP_ROOT=/Volumes/Data/tmp/libra-1055-tc
export GIT_BUILD_DIR="$TC_TMP_ROOT/git-build-6de20f6092"
bash cases/lib/build-git-control.sh
test "$("$GIT_BUILD_DIR/bin-wrappers/git" --version)" = "git version 2.56.0.116.g6de20f6092"
cd /Volumes/Data/gitmono/libra
cargo build --bin libra
test "$(cargo metadata --no-deps --format-version 1 | jq -er .target_directory)" = /Volumes/Data/gitmono/libra/target
test "$(target/debug/libra --version)" = "libra $(cargo metadata --no-deps --format-version 1 | jq -er '.packages[] | select(.name == "libra") | .version')"
cd /Volumes/Data/gitmono/libra-testcases
run_1055_tc() {
  local track="$1" output
  export TC_TRACK="$track"
  case "$track" in
    git) export LIBRA_BIN="$GIT_BUILD_DIR/bin-wrappers/git" ;;
    libra) export LIBRA_BIN=/Volumes/Data/gitmono/libra/target/debug/libra ;;
    *) echo "unknown track $track" >&2; return 1 ;;
  esac
  TC_RUN_ID=$(basename "$(mktemp -d "$TC_TMP_ROOT/i1055-${track}-XXXXXX")") || return 1
  export TC_RUN_ID
  output=$(bash /Volumes/Data/gitmono/libra-testcases/cases/tc3285-cat-file-p/*.sh 3285) || return 1
  printf '%s\n' "$output"
  [ "$(printf '%s\n' "$output" | grep -cF "# track=$track target=$LIBRA_BIN ")" -ge 1 ] || return 1
  [ "$(printf '%s\n' "$output" | grep -cE '^TC-3285 PASS([ :]|$)')" -ge 1 ] || return 1
}
```

## 实施顺序

`I1055-01 -> I1055-02 -> I1055-03`。I1055-01 交付共享切分与 rev-parse/cat-file；I1055-02 只接 show 的四个路径；I1055-03 只接 ls-tree。02 只能在 01 `done`/`complete`（D 全绿且终止链提交已推送）后开工，03 同样只能在 02 之后；三卡共写 `COMPATIBILITY.md`（各写各的行）与 `src/utils/util.rs` 提供的共享切分（02、03 消费 01 的 helper），因此全串行。后卡不得回退前卡语义。

### 发布分组与并发窗口

三卡各自 independent、patch + 1，顺序 01→02→03，每卡在前一卡终止链提交推送后才 bump；跨计划窗口见 DEP-I1055-12。执行本计划者是唯一发布者。

**并发声明：** 本计划的卡与 issues/1050–1057 的其它卡全串行（DEP-I1055-12，`plan-status.md` 第六节 DEP-I1050-1057-REL）：它们共写 `COMPATIBILITY.md`（各写各的行）、网站 `cf` 分支、版本面与 `plan-status.md`，同一时刻只允许一张卡处于 `in-progress`，前一卡的终止链提交推送后下一卡才开工。与其它计划的写集互斥见依赖登记表与开工门。

### Phase 0 / Phase 1

先满足 DEP 并执行写集扫描。CF-03 若已完成，只刷新其 `split_tree_path_spec` 或等价 helper，不回退已落地语义。

## 开工门

**开工门（`bash` 运行，`CARD` 为本卡 ID）：** 脚本先 fetch，并断言工作区干净、`HEAD` 等于 `origin/main`（或者是父提交为 `origin/main`、只改本文件与 `plan-status.md` 的单父提交，也就是计划目录推送脚本推送前重跑本门时的开工登记），所以扫描的是远端最新的计划文本；然后做四件事，任一不满足即非零退出，本卡保持 `pending`，不写 `in-progress`、不提交开工登记。
1. 扫描 `docs/development/plan` 下全部计划（本文件、`plan-status.md`、`plan-template.md` 除外），按 `##`、`###`、`####` 三级的 `Task ` 标题切卡。任何处于 `in-progress` 或 `blocked` 的卡，只要 Implementation write set 命中本计划写集，或根本没有 Implementation write set 字段，或该字段为空，就是活动写者。写集取从 `**Implementation write set:**` 起、到下一个加粗字段或标题之前的整段，换行后的列表也算在内；写集文字提到 Deliverables（例如「同 Deliverables」）时，同卡 `**Deliverables:**` 字段的内容一并计入；解析后既没有反引号中的路径、也不以 `N/A` 开头的写集，无法证明不相交，同样算活动写者。活动与否按白名单判定，不按黑名单：卡内第一条加粗 `Lifecycle / Acceptance` 字段行的首词（去掉反引号）是 `pending`、`done`、`released`、`deferred`、`废弃`、`取消` 或 `合并回`，且第一个括号之前的 Acceptance 半边不是 `locally-accepted` 或 `remote-pending` 时，该卡不活动；其余一律算活动卡，包括 `in-progress`、`blocked`、写在 Lifecycle 位上的 `locally-accepted`（2026-11-07 的实例：`plan-20260926.md:972`、`plan-20261018-grok-capture.md:486` 等，以及远端 `5ea34fd` 中旧文本的 I1052-00）、`planned`、`done` / `remote-pending`、大小写或连字符不规范的写法（如 `In-Progress`、`in_progress`）、空首词，以及没有可解析字段行的卡。`blocked` 卡只在字段行以两种固定形态之一开头时不算活动：`blocked（superseded：`，或 `` `blocked` / 空（ `` 之后 20 字以内出现「本卡已正式延后」（2026-11-07 符合的是 `plan-20260825.md:643`、`:1003`、`:1094` 与 `plan-20260729.md:7578`）；行内其它位置出现 `superseded` 或「已正式延后」不算。脚本扫描之前先用十条样例行自测这一判定（覆盖上述活动与不活动形态），任一判定与预期不符即非零退出。命中有四种方式：正文匹配本计划写集文件的模式（行号的 `:217` 与「（第 217 行）」两种写法都能匹配）；`COMPATIBILITY.md` 的行号区间（如 `:200-230`、「第 200–230 行」）覆盖本计划登记的行；反引号中的通配写集（如 `src/command/*.rs`，花括号 `{a,b}` 先展开）或目录写集（如 `docs/commands/`）覆盖本计划的任一文件（网站页按 `../libra-backend/`、`/Volumes/Sea/libra-backend/` 两种前缀归一后比较）；写集里出现不带数字行号的 `COMPATIBILITY.md`、且同一写集提到本计划的命令名（`rev-parse`、`cat-file`、`show`、`ls-tree`，按完整命令名匹配）时也算命中；只写文件名或写别的命令行（例如「`COMPATIBILITY.md` 的 fetch 行」）的写集改的是其它命令的行，与本计划登记的行不相交。它若插入或删除行，让本计划登记的行号漂移，开工检查与暂存守卫的行身份检查（行首前缀在全文恰好命中一次且位于登记行号）会失败并停止，按 ER-10 修订行号，所以不需要在开工门里把它当作写者；
2. 自检：卡外的 `Lifecycle / Acceptance` 行（加粗或不加粗）若是 `in-progress` 或 `blocked` 也算失败；卡内第一行之后若还有加粗的活动 `Lifecycle / Acceptance` 字段行，同样失败。这两种情况说明有标题格式不同的卡没被切出来。卡内不加粗的历史日志行（例如 `plan-20260904.md:4665`）不计。
3. issues/1050–1057 的其它计划中，任何一张卡不是 `pending`（Acceptance 半边不是 `locally-accepted` 或 `remote-pending`），也不是 `done` / `complete`，都失败（全串行，`plan-status.md` 第六节 DEP-I1050-1057-REL）；这一项比第 1 项更严，`released`、`deferred`、`blocked` 的豁免形态与无法解析的卡在家族内一律失败。
4. 本卡的前置：`card` 类前置必须是 `done` / `complete`；`accepted` 类前置必须是 `in-progress` / `locally-accepted`；`ex` 类前置在本文件 waiver 表中的 Approver 列必须恰好是「`` `<GitHub login>` ``（`YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM:SS UTC`，`<AC 分子>/<VER 分子>`）」，分子与粒度审计表中该卡的 AC、VER 分子一致，「Review round / 证据」列不为空、不含「待」，且该 login 在 `libra-tools/libra` 上的角色是 `admin` 或 `maintain`（`gh api` 查询 `role_name`；GitHub 对 maintain 角色的 `permission` 字段返回 `write`，所以不用该字段）。

前置未满足时本卡保持 `pending`，开工门非零退出并上报；`blocked` 只用于已经 `in-progress` 之后的 ER-10 越界故障（`plan-template.md:243`）。

```bash
remote_main=$(libra rev-parse origin/main)
test -n "$remote_main"
head=$(libra rev-parse HEAD)
if [ "$head" != "$remote_main" ]; then

  test "$(libra rev-parse HEAD^)" = "$remote_main"

  test -z "$(printf '%s\n' "$own" | grep -vxF -e docs/development/plan/issues/1055.md -e docs/development/plan/plan-status.md || true)"
fi

repo = Path('/Volumes/Data/gitmono/libra')
root = repo / 'docs/development/plan'
card = os.environ['CARD']
head = re.compile(r'(?m)^#{2,4} Task ')
lc_field = re.compile(r'(?m)^\*\*Lifecycle / Acceptance:\*\*[ \t]*`?([^\s/`|（(]*)[^\n]*$')
lc_any = re.compile(r'(?m)^[ \t>*-]*\**Lifecycle / Acceptance\**[:：]\**[ \t]*`?([^\s/`|（(]*)[^\n]*$')
ws = re.compile(r'(?ms)^\*\*Implementation write set:\*\*(.*?)(?=^\*\*[^*\n]{1,80}[:：]\*\*|^#{1,6} |\Z)')
tick = re.compile(r'`([^`\s]+)`')
dl = re.compile(r'(?ms)\*\*Deliverables:\*\*(.*?)(?=\*\*[^*\n]{1,80}[:：]\*\*|^#{1,6} |\Z)')
crange = re.compile(r'COMPATIBILITY\.md`?[ \t]*(?::|（第|第)[ \t]*(\d+)[ \t]*[-–~至到][ \t]*(\d+)')
compat_bare = re.compile(r'COMPATIBILITY\.md(?!`?[ \t]*(?::[ \t]*\d|（第[ \t]*\d|第[ \t]*\d))')
needles = [re.compile(n) for n in cfg['needles']]
files = cfg['files']
family = re.compile(r'^issues/105[0-7](?:-[a-z0-9]+)*\.md$')
ACTIVE = ('in-progress', 'blocked')
INACTIVE = ('pending', 'done', 'released', 'deferred', '废弃', '取消', '合并回', 'legacy-closed', 'withdrawn', 'registered')
EXEMPT = re.compile(r'^\*\*Lifecycle / Acceptance:\*\*[ \t]*(?:blocked（superseded：|`blocked` / 空（[^）\n]{0,20}本卡已正式延后)')
IN_FLIGHT = re.compile(r'/[ \t]*`?(?:locally-accepted|remote-pending)(?![\w-])')
COMPLETE = re.compile(r'/[ \t]*`?complete(?![\w-])')

def before_paren(line):
    return re.split(r'[（(]', line, maxsplit=1)[0]

def active(state, line):
    if state == 'blocked' and EXEMPT.match(line):
        return False
    return not (state in INACTIVE and not IN_FLIGHT.search(before_paren(line)))

def family_idle(state, line):
    head_part = before_paren(line)
    if state == 'pending':
        return not IN_FLIGHT.search(head_part)
    return state == 'done' and bool(COMPLETE.search(head_part))

SELFTEST = [
    ('**Lifecycle / Acceptance:** locally-accepted / 空（ER-05 复审待完成）', True),
    ('**Lifecycle / Acceptance:** in_progress / 空', True),
    ('**Lifecycle / Acceptance:** In-Progress / 空', True),
    ('**Lifecycle / Acceptance:** planned / 空', True),
    ('**Lifecycle / Acceptance:** `done` / `remote-pending`', True),
    ('**Lifecycle / Acceptance:** blocked / 空（待 superseded 判定）', True),
    ('**Lifecycle / Acceptance:** pending / 空', False),
    ('**Lifecycle / Acceptance:** `done` / `complete`（曾为 remote-pending）', False),
    ('**Lifecycle / Acceptance:** blocked（superseded：由 X 承接）', False),
    ('**Lifecycle / Acceptance:** `blocked` / 空（ER-04 闭集；本卡已正式延后）', False),
]
lc_inline = re.compile(r'(?m)^[^\n]*?\**(?:Historical )?Lifecycle / Acceptance\**[:：]\**[ \t]*`?([^\s/`|（(。]*)([^\n]*)$')
lc_split = re.compile(r'(?m)^[ \t>*-]*\*\*Lifecycle:\*\*[ \t]*`?([^\s/`|（(]*)`?[ \t]*\|[ \t]*\*\*Acceptance:\*\*[ \t]*`?([^\s`|（(]*)')
LEGACY = ('plan-20260708.md', 'plan-20260713.md', 'plan-20260714.md', 'plan-20260715.md')
REGISTERED = {('issues/477.md', 'HW-0%d' % k): '?' for k in range(1, 8)}
REGISTERED[('plan-20261018-grok-capture.md', 'GK-08')] = 'planned'
withdrawn = re.compile(r'(?m)^\*\*Scope disposition:\*\*[ \t]*withdrawn(?![\w-])')
status_text = (root / 'plan-status.md').read_text()

def legacy_closed(rel, text):
    if rel not in LEGACY or lc_any.search(text) or lc_inline.search(text):
        return False
    rows = [l for l in status_text.splitlines() if l.startswith('| [`' + rel + '`](' + rel + ')')]
    return len(rows) == 1 and '**已收口**' in rows[0]

def parse_state(rel, body, fields, text):
    if fields:
        return fields[0].group(1), fields[0].group(0)
    if withdrawn.search(body):
        return 'withdrawn', ''
    m = lc_inline.search(body)
    if m:
        return m.group(1), '**Lifecycle / Acceptance:** ' + m.group(0)[m.start(1) - m.start(0):]
    m = lc_split.search(body)
    if m:
        return m.group(1), '**Lifecycle / Acceptance:** ' + m.group(1) + ' / ' + m.group(2)
    if legacy_closed(rel, text):
        return 'legacy-closed', ''
    return '?', ''

def card_state(rel, cid, body, fields, text):
    state, line = parse_state(rel, body, fields, text)
    if REGISTERED.get((rel, cid)) == state and not IN_FLIGHT.search(before_paren(line)):
        return 'registered', line
    return state, line

for sample, want in SELFTEST:
    m = lc_field.search(sample)
    got = active(m.group(1), m.group(0)) if m else True
    if got != want:
        sys.exit(f'start gate self-test failed: {sample!r} active={got}, expected {want}')
problems = []
cards = {}

def expand(token):
    m = re.search(r'\{([^{}]*)\}', token)
    if not m:
        return [token]
    out = []
    for part in m.group(1).split(','):
        out.extend(expand(token[:m.start()] + part + token[m.end():]))
    return out

def hits(text):
    if any(n.search(text) for n in needles):
        return 'pattern'
    for m in crange.finditer(text):
        lo, hi = int(m.group(1)), int(m.group(2))
        if any(lo <= n <= hi for n in cfg['lines']):
            return 'COMPATIBILITY.md range ' + m.group(0)
    if cfg['compat'] and compat_bare.search(text) and any(re.search(r'(?<![\w-])' + re.escape(c) + r'(?![\w-])', text) for c in cfg['commands']):
        return 'COMPATIBILITY.md without a line number'
    for raw in [x for t in tick.findall(text) for x in expand(t)]:
        token = raw.split('：', 1)[0]
        token = re.sub(r':\d[\d,\-–]*$', '', token)
        while token.startswith('./'):
            token = token[2:]
        token = re.sub(r'^(?:\.\./|/Volumes/(?:Sea|Data/gitmono)/)libra-backend/', 'BACKEND/', token)
        if not token:
            continue
        if token != 'COMPATIBILITY.md' and (token in files or any(n.search(token) for n in needles)):
            return 'path ' + raw
        if any(c in token for c in '*?['):
            if any(fnmatch.fnmatchcase(f, token) for f in files):
                return 'glob ' + raw
        elif token.endswith('/') or (repo / token).is_dir():
            prefix = token.rstrip('/') + '/'
            if any(f.startswith(prefix) for f in files):
                return 'directory ' + raw
    return ''

for path in sorted(root.rglob('*.md')):
    rel = path.relative_to(root).as_posix()
    if rel in ('plan-status.md', 'plan-template.md'):
        continue
    text = path.read_text(errors='replace')
    parts = head.split(text)
    for m in lc_any.finditer(parts[0]):
        if active(m.group(1), '**Lifecycle / Acceptance:** ' + m.group(0)[m.start(1) - m.start(0):]):
            problems.append(f'{rel}: active Lifecycle line outside a parsed Task card')
    for body in parts[1:]:
        cid = re.split(r'[:：\s]', body, maxsplit=1)[0]
        fields = list(lc_field.finditer(body))
        if cid == 'cards' and not fields:
            continue
        state, line = card_state(rel, cid, body, fields, text)
        cards[(rel, cid)] = (state, line)
        if any(active(m.group(1), m.group(0)) for m in fields[1:]):
            problems.append(f'{rel}: {cid} has a second active Lifecycle field (unparsed card heading?)')
        if rel == cfg['self']:
            continue
        if family.match(rel):
            if not family_idle(state, line):
                problems.append(f'{rel}: {cid} is {state or "(empty)"} (issues/1050-1057 run strictly one card at a time; other cards must be pending or done/complete)')
            continue
        if not active(state, line):
            continue
        w = ws.search(body)
        if not w or not w.group(1).strip():
            problems.append(f'{rel}: {cid} is {state} without a non-empty Implementation write set')
            continue
        text = w.group(1)
        if 'Deliverables' in text:
            d = dl.search(body)
            text = text + ' ' + (d.group(1) if d else '')
        if not tick.search(text) and not text.strip().startswith('N/A') and not any(n.search(text) for n in needles):
            problems.append(f'{rel}: {cid} is {state} and its write set names no file paths')
            continue
        why = hits(text)
        if why:
            problems.append(f'{rel}: {cid} is {state} and writes this plan\'s files ({why})')

self_text = (root / cfg['self']).read_text()

def audit_numerators(task):
    for l in self_text.splitlines():
        cols = [c.strip() for c in l.split('|')]
        if len(cols) > 9 and cols[1] == task and cols[2] in ('implementation', 'audit', 'release'):
            ac = re.match(r'(\d+)/', cols[7]); ver = re.match(r'(\d+)/', cols[8])
            if ac and ver:
                return ac.group(1), ver.group(1)
    return None

approver_re = re.compile(r'^`([A-Za-z0-9](?:[A-Za-z0-9-]{0,38}))`（(\d{4}-\d\d-\d\d \d\d:\d\d:\d\d) UTC，(\d+)/(\d+)）$')
for req in cfg['prereqs'].get(card, []):
    if req[0] in ('card', 'accepted'):
        state, line = cards.get((req[1], req[2]), ('missing', ''))
        if req[0] == 'card' and (state != 'done' or 'complete' not in line):
            problems.append(f'prerequisite {req[1]} {req[2]} is not done/complete: {state}')
        if req[0] == 'accepted' and (state != 'in-progress' or 'locally-accepted' not in line):
            problems.append(f'prerequisite {req[1]} {req[2]} is not in-progress/locally-accepted: {state}')
    elif req[0] == 'ex':
        rows = [l for l in self_text.splitlines() if l.startswith('| ' + req[1] + ' |')]
        cols = [c.strip() for c in rows[0].split('|')] if len(rows) == 1 else []
        if len(cols) < 8:
            problems.append(f'{req[1]}: waiver row missing or malformed')
            continue
        m = approver_re.match(cols[5])
        nums = audit_numerators(cols[2])
        if not m:
            problems.append(f'{req[1]} is not approved in the required format: {cols[5]}')
        elif nums is None or (m.group(3), m.group(4)) != nums:
            problems.append(f'{req[1]} approval {m.group(3)}/{m.group(4)} does not match the audit table {nums}')
        if not cols[6] or '待' in cols[6]:
            problems.append(f'{req[1]} review round is not recorded: {cols[6]}')
        if m:
            r = subprocess.run(['gh', 'api', f'repos/libra-tools/libra/collaborators/{m.group(1)}/permission', '--jq', '.role_name'],
                               capture_output=True, text=True)
            if r.returncode != 0 or r.stdout.strip() not in ('admin', 'maintain'):
                problems.append(f'{req[1]} approver {m.group(1)} is not an admin/maintain collaborator: {r.stdout.strip() or r.stderr.strip()}')
if card not in cfg['prereqs']:
    problems.append(f'unknown CARD={card}')
for p in problems:
    print('start gate:', p)
sys.exit(1 if problems else 0)
GATE
if [ "$CARD" = I1055-01 ]; then
  count=$(rg -c 'pub\(crate\) fn split_tree_path_spec\(name: &str\) -> Option<\(&str, &str\)>' src/utils/util.rs)
  test "$count" = 1
fi
```

**外部前置检查（`bash` 运行，`CARD` 为本卡 ID，`WHEN` 取 `start`、`stage1` 或 `stage3`）：** 依赖登记表中的网站与 TC 前置在这里机械检查，不留给执行者目测。开工门通过之后、写 `in-progress` 之前以 `WHEN=start` 运行；`WHEN=stage1` 在 C 组提交之前（生命周期第 3 步第 1 小步）与发布四段第一段之前各运行一次，第三段之前以 `WHEN=stage3` 再运行一次。`WHEN=start` 失败时本卡保持 `pending`，不提交、不推送开工登记；C 组提交之前的 `stage1` 与第三段之前的 `stage3` 失败时不推送、不发布，按生命周期第 8 步登记 `blocked`；第一段之前重复的 `stage1` 失败时只停止并报告（C 组提交尚未推送，不做登记）；前置恢复后回到 `in-progress` 并重跑本检查。共同部分：`gh` 已认证。TC 部分（有 TC 门的卡，只在 `WHEN=start` 检查，因为 TC 门在 C 组内运行，第一段与第三段时早已跑完；TC 门块自己在运行时再核对一次固定提交）：testcase 仓库 `/Volumes/Data/gitmono/libra-testcases` 含固定提交 `b8ad10bd14d95ffc03cd55927a37f31e67d8ff42`（`TC=head` 的卡还要求它的 `HEAD` 就是该提交，且 `git status --porcelain` 为空，因为 harness、补丁与 TC 脚本都从这个工作区执行；`TC=pin` 的卡只比对固定提交里的 TSV、不构建也不运行 TC，只检查到这里为止），控制构建脚本存在，Git 源 `/Volumes/Data/gitmono/git` 含 `6de20f6092dcf9bdb1c8efe03db4b70c82b423dd`，构建所需的 `make`、`cc`、harness 需要的 GNU `timeout` 或 `gtimeout`（`cases/lib/tc-harness.sh` 缺它会退出）与本卡额外工具可用。网站部分（有网站面的卡）：`/Volumes/Sea/libra-backend` 存在且是该真实路径，有 `.libra`、没有 `.git`，当前分支 `cf`，工作区干净，fetch 后 `HEAD` 等于 `origin/cf`，同步脚本存在，`pnpm` 可用，`wrangler whoami` 报告已登录；同步脚本对本卡每个命令以检查模式运行，`WHEN=start` 与 `WHEN=stage3` 接受退出码 0 或 1，`WHEN=stage1` 只接受 1（C 组已改源文档、网站尚未同步），退出码 2（同步拒绝）一律失败。网站部分只 fetch，不 pull，不写后端；`HEAD` 不等于 `origin/cf` 时失败，由执行者在干净的后端工作区先执行 `libra pull --ff-only` 再重跑。

```bash
set -eo pipefail
cd /Volumes/Data/gitmono/libra
case "${CARD:?export CARD first}" in
  I1055-01) CMDS='rev-parse cat-file'; TC='head'; TOOLS='' ;;
  I1055-02) CMDS='show'; TC=''; TOOLS='' ;;
  I1055-03) CMDS='ls-tree'; TC=''; TOOLS='' ;;
  *) echo "unknown CARD=$CARD" >&2; exit 1 ;;
esac
case "${WHEN:?export WHEN=start, stage1 or stage3}" in start|stage1|stage3) ;; *) echo "unknown WHEN=$WHEN" >&2; exit 1 ;; esac
gh auth status >/dev/null
if [ -n "$TC" ] && [ "$WHEN" = start ]; then
  tc_repo=/Volumes/Data/gitmono/libra-testcases
  pin=b8ad10bd14d95ffc03cd55927a37f31e67d8ff42
  git -C "$tc_repo" cat-file -e "$pin^{commit}"
  if [ "$TC" = head ]; then test "$(git -C "$tc_repo" rev-parse HEAD)" = "$pin"; fi
  if [ "$TC" = head ]; then test -z "$(git -C "$tc_repo" status --porcelain)"; fi
fi
if [ -n "$TC" ] && [ "$TC" != pin ] && [ "$WHEN" = start ]; then
  test -f "$tc_repo/cases/lib/build-git-control.sh"
  git -C /Volumes/Data/gitmono/git cat-file -e '6de20f6092dcf9bdb1c8efe03db4b70c82b423dd^{commit}'
  for tool in make cc $TOOLS; do command -v "$tool" >/dev/null; done
  if ! command -v timeout >/dev/null; then command -v gtimeout >/dev/null; fi
fi
if [ -n "$CMDS" ]; then
  backend=/Volumes/Sea/libra-backend
  test -d "$backend"
  cd "$backend"
  test "$(pwd -P)" = "$backend"
  test -d .libra
  test ! -e .git
  test "$(libra branch --show-current)" = cf
  status=$(libra status --porcelain --untracked-files=all)
  test -z "$status"
  libra fetch origin
  remote_cf=$(libra rev-parse origin/cf)
  test -n "$remote_cf"
  head=$(libra rev-parse HEAD)
  if [ "$head" != "$remote_cf" ]; then echo "backend cf is not at origin/cf; run 'libra pull --ff-only' in the clean backend and rerun" >&2; exit 1; fi
  test -f scripts/sync-libra-command-docs.py
  command -v pnpm >/dev/null
  who=$(pnpm --filter @tinyship/tanstack-app exec wrangler whoami 2>&1)
  test "$(printf '%s\n' "$who" | grep -cF 'You are logged in')" -ge 1
  for command in $CMDS; do
    set +e
    python3 scripts/sync-libra-command-docs.py --libra-root /Volumes/Data/gitmono/libra --only "$command" >/dev/null
    rc=$?
    set -e
    case "$WHEN:$rc" in
      start:0|start:1|stage3:0|stage3:1|stage1:1) ;;
      *) echo "sync check for $command exited $rc at WHEN=$WHEN" >&2; exit 1 ;;
    esac
  done
fi
echo "external prerequisites OK for $CARD at WHEN=$WHEN"
```

## 任务卡

### 字段全局默认与例外

implementation、independent、immutable-release（发布后不撤回，只发新 patch 前滚；推送前按生命周期第 9 步放弃）。EX-I1055-01 尚未批准，计划审查不等于批准（DEP-I1055-11）。

| 例外 ID | 任务 | 豁免项 | 理由与补偿措施 | Approver | Review round / 证据 | 有效期 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| EX-I1055-01 | I1055-01 | G-03 门族型 AC / Verification 上限 | 同一「括号深度切分」轴上的 20 个判据与 14 个具名门（4 个 lib 测试、8 个 command_test 测试、TC-3285 两轨 2 门）；每门都是卡内判据规范块里的可复制命令，失败即整卡不达标；切分规则、它在 rev-parse/cat-file 上的直接效果与错误消息是同一规则的后果，拆开会产生没有独立恢复轴的碎片卡（原 I1055-04 即因此并回） | 待维护者具名批准，当前未批准 | 本轮计划审查待定 | 本卡；数量变化须重审 |

按 `plan-template.md:583`，EX 具名批准到位之前，审计表对本卡判「不达标」，本卡不得开工；这由依赖登记表中的审批 DEP 与开工门第 4 项机械拦住。本计划审查只核对 waiver 的准入条件（白名单项、卡内判据规范块、如实分子、有效期），不代替批准。

| 任务 | type | axis | recovery | complete | self-contained | AC | VER | landing / prod-files | scope | deps | writeset | release | split-from | exception |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| I1055-01 | implementation | 共享切分 | immutable-release | yes | yes | 20/8@EX-I1055-01 | 14/8@EX-I1055-01 | 1/1 | M | DEP-I1055-SCAN + DEP-I1055-01..03,07..12 | 序列化于 02、03 | independent | N/A | EX-I1055-01 待批准 |
| I1055-02 | implementation | show 消费 | immutable-release | yes | yes | 8/8 | 8/8 | 1/1 | M | DEP-I1055-SCAN + I1055-01,DEP-I1055-01,05,07,09,12 | 序列化于 01、03 | independent | 自 I1055-01 拆出 | N/A |
| I1055-03 | implementation | ls-tree 消费 | immutable-release | yes | yes | 8/8 | 5/8 | 1/1 | M | DEP-I1055-SCAN + I1055-02,DEP-I1055-01,04,07,09,12 | 序列化于 01、02 | independent | 自 I1055-01 拆出 | N/A |

各卡另有 ER-06/06a 文档门，不计入 AC。

### Task I1055-01: 修改共享 `split_tree_path_spec`

**Task type:** implementation

**Lifecycle / Acceptance:** pending / 空

**Description:** 在 #537 CF-03 已抽出的 `pub(crate) fn split_tree_path_spec(name: &str) -> Option<(&str, &str)>` 上替换首个冒号规则（ADR-I1055-01）。rev-parse 与 cat-file 已调用共享解析器，本卡不改这两个命令源文件。

**Out of scope:** show 与 ls-tree 的私有切分；新正则方言。

**Current evidence:** 当前 `src/utils/util.rs:3075-3080`；CF-03 计划将其提取为 helper。Git `object-name.c:1822-1830`（`6de20f6092`）。

**Acceptance criteria:**

- [ ] `split_tree_path_spec("HEAD^{/foo}^{/bar:}:hello")` 返回 `Some(("HEAD^{/foo}^{/bar:}", "hello"))`。
- [ ] `split_tree_path_spec(":/testing:")` 返回 `None`。
- [ ] `split_tree_path_spec("foo{bar:hello")` 返回 `Some(("foo{bar", "hello"))`：裸 `{` 不增加深度。
- [ ] `split_tree_path_spec("foo@@{0}:hello")` 返回 `Some(("foo@@{0}", "hello"))`：`@{` 增加深度，`}` 归零后的冒号是分隔符。
- [ ] `cat-file -p 'other^{/testing:}:hello'` 的 stdout 等于 `cat-file -p HEAD:hello`。
- [ ] fixture 文件 `a}b` 内容 `brace` 时，`rev-parse 'HEAD:a}b'` 的对象 ID 等于 `hash-object` 对该内容的结果。
- [ ] `rev-parse 'HEAD^{/testing:}:missing'` 以非零退出。
- [ ] 同一命令的 stderr 含 `path 'missing' does not exist in`。
- [ ] `rev-parse 'HEAD^{/no-such:}'` 以非零退出。
- [ ] 同一命令的 stderr 含 `invalid reference: HEAD^{/no-such:}`。
- [ ] 未闭合的 `rev-parse 'HEAD^{/testing:'` 以非零退出。
- [ ] 同一命令的 stderr 含 `invalid reference: HEAD^{/testing:`；这是切分改变后的结果，不是基线消息。
- [ ] TC-3285 的 Git 轨打印 `TC-3285 PASS`。
- [ ] TC-3285 的 Libra 轨打印 `TC-3285 PASS`。
- [ ] 否定形式内的冒号不切路径：`rev-parse 'HEAD^{/!-testing:}'` 的 stdout 等于 `rev-parse HEAD`（HEAD 的消息不含 `testing:`；issue 验收 2）。
- [ ] `!!` 保留字面 `!`：`rev-parse 'HEAD^{/!!testing:}'` 以非零退出（没有提交消息含 `!testing:`；若被误当作否定会命中 HEAD）。
- [ ] 同一命令的 stderr 含 `not a valid object name`（issue 验收 3，与 `:/` 的诊断一致）。
- [ ] 含冒号的非法正则 `cat-file -t 'HEAD^{/(:}'` 以非零退出。

### Task I1055-03: ls-tree 调用 `split_tree_path_spec`

**Task type:** implementation

**Lifecycle / Acceptance:** pending / 空

**Description:** 非 `:/` 的 treeish 调用 I1055-01 的 helper。以 `:/` 开头时保持当前 `split_once(':')` 和空 rev 的 ambiguous 错误，不新增 `:/` 支持。含消息搜索 `^{/` 的 treeish 由严格解析器解析失败时直接报错，不再落到 `storage.search_result` 的旧式导航（`src/command/ls_tree.rs:243-247`；`src/utils/client_storage.rs` 的旧式导航对含 `^` 的字符串用未锚定的 `(\^|~)(\d*)` 匹配，会把 `^{/no-such}` 当成 `^1`，2026-11-07 在 0.30.158 上实测 `ls-tree 'HEAD^{/no-such}'` 退出 0 并列出 `HEAD~1` 的树）；其它输入保留现有路径，本卡不改：对象 ID 前缀依赖该回落；`^{tree}` 等类型剥离目前同样被严格解析器拒绝（它只接受能剥成提交的说明）后落到旧式导航，2026-11-07 在已安装的 0.30.157 上实测 `ls-tree --name-only 'HEAD^{tree}'` 退出 0 但列出父提交的树（`rev-parse 'HEAD^{tree}'` 与 `HEAD~1^{tree}` 是两棵不同的树），这是与冒号切分无关的既有缺陷，登记 DEFER-I1055-03，不借本卡修复。

**Out of scope:** 目录过滤、大小格式（#1057）、消息搜索。

**Current evidence:** `src/command/ls_tree.rs:213`。

**Acceptance criteria:**

- [ ] `ls-tree 'HEAD^{/testing:}:dir'` 的 stdout 等于 `ls-tree 'HEAD:dir'`。
- [ ] `ls-tree ':/testing:'` 保持基线退出码 129。
- [ ] 同一命令的 stderr 含 `ambiguous tree-ish: ''`。
- [ ] 同一命令的 stderr 含 `LBR-CLI-003`。
- [ ] `ls-tree 'HEAD^{/testing:}:missing'` 以非零退出。
- [ ] 同一命令的 stderr 含 `path 'missing' does not exist in the selected tree`。
- [ ] `ls-tree 'HEAD^{/no-such:}:dir'` 以 129 退出（无匹配的消息搜索不回落到旧式导航，与 `rev-parse` 对同一输入的退出码相同）。
- [ ] `docs/development/commands/ls-tree.md` 不再含「在首个 `:` 处切分」（本卡特有的文档语义判据，按 `plan-template.md:331` 计入；由「文档门」的 `I1055-03` 分支判定）。
- [ ] ER-06/06a 同卡同步门（不计入 AC 分子）：`docs/commands/ls-tree.md`、`docs/commands/zh-CN/ls-tree.md`、`docs/development/commands/ls-tree.md`（同时删除 `:61` 的「在首个 `:` 处切分」）、`COMPATIBILITY.md:203` 与网站 `ls-tree.en.md` 都更新，三份命令文档各自单独成行写入精确句，「文档门」与网站三段通过。精确句不承载新的行为语义：它复述的行为已由本卡的 AC 与测试判定，句子只用于机械证明仓库文档与线上页面已经同步。

## 共享夹具

所有命令测试使用同一临时仓库：
- 根提交 `base`，消息 `base`，文件 `hello` 内容 `hello`，目录 `dir` 含 `file` 内容 `nested`，文件 `a}b` 内容 `brace`。
- 提交 `other` 是 `base` 的子提交，消息是 `testing: just a bunch of junk`，树含 `hello`、`dir/file` 和 `a}b`；分支 `other` 指向该提交。上游 t1006 的 `other` 由 `commit-tree` 生成、没有父提交；这里改为 `base` 的子提交，只为让 `HEAD^{/testing:}` 能沿祖先命中，消息与树内容和上游一致。
- HEAD 是 `other` 的后代。HEAD 最新提交消息是 `unique-head-sample`，不含 `testing:`，树与 `other` 相同，提交时间更晚。因此 `HEAD^{/testing:}` 沿祖先命中 `other`，`other^{/testing:}` 命中 `other` 自己，`:/unique-head` 只命中 HEAD。
- 原 I1055-04 的 `foo{bar`、`foo@@{0}` 判据改为 I1055-01 的纯单元断言（AC3、AC4），不再需要对应分支与 reflog。

## 里程碑验收与回滚

A/B、Codex ER-05 PASS、C、`remote-pending`、D 全绿、终止链提交之后才算 `done`/`complete`，见「生命周期」。T-1 全量失败时不得标记 `locally-accepted`。已发布的 tag 不改。

### 故障恢复矩阵

| 事件 | 动作 |
|---|---|
| 切分回归 | 修复后重跑 lib 与 rev-parse 门 |
| 全量暴露无关失败 | 修复或证明既有并记录；不跳过 T-1 |
| CF-03 或扫描命中的卡正在写同一文件 | 开工前：开工门拦住，保持 `pending`；开工后才出现：`blocked`（ER-10） |
| 上游前进 | 开工前：开工检查在干净工作区里 `libra pull --ff-only`；bump 前：`libra pull --ff-only --autostash`（只涉及本卡改动）；C 组提交之后：发布四段开头的有界恢复（rebase、重签，上游有任何增量都整套重跑 C 组，最多 3 次）；开工登记、`remote-pending` 登记或终止链提交：计划目录推送脚本的有界恢复（最多 3 次） |
| 工作区出现本卡允许集合以外的改动 | 工作区独占检查失败，停止；不提交、移动或清理他人改动；开工前保持 `pending`；开工后按 ER-10 停止并报告，不做 `blocked` 登记（登记提交无法与他人改动隔离），本卡保持 `in-progress`，对其它计划的开工门同样算活动写者 |
| 外部前置长期不可用，或用户决定改期 | 本卡没有推送过 C 组提交时按生命周期第 9 步放弃：撤销本卡改动，回到 `pending` / 空，释放家族串行锁；已推送的卡不能放弃，只能修复或前滚 |
| 推送报错 | 先看远端：fetch 后 `origin/main` 等于该 SHA 或 GitHub compare 显示它已在 `main` 上，就算推送成功，原样重跑脚本即可确认；`origin/main` 未动是认证、网络或服务端错误，按 ER-09 不重试，记录错误后报告；已前进到别处进入有界恢复 |
| 候选分支 CI 重跑一次后在建 tag 之前仍失败 | 先按 ER-13 在本地复现并归因。与本卡有关时进入生命周期第 6 步修复模式：不 bump，在本卡写集内修复，重走 C 组与第一段（`MODE=repair`，修复 SHA 快进推上同一候选分支），在修复 SHA 上建原版本的 tag；与本卡无关时保持 `in-progress` / `remote-pending` 并向用户报告，不在写集外修改 |
| C 组本地门失败（Verification 门、fmt、Clippy、doc、全量 nextest 或 release 构建） | 先归因：本卡引入的就在写集内修复，再由 C 组运行器整套重跑；在 bump 前的基线上也能复现的既有失败，按 ER-13 登记 FIX- 或 DEFER- 并向用户报告，不在写集外修改，本卡保持 `in-progress` / `locally-accepted`，需要等待时按生命周期第 8 步登记 `blocked` |
| 网站段一在 `--write` 之后失败（类型检查、构建或页面核对） | 后端工作区留下本卡命令页的生成改动：只用 `libra restore` 还原这些 `apps/tanstack-app/content/docs/commands/<命令>.en.md` 页面，确认后端工作区干净后修正原因再重跑段一；失败的生成结果不提交、不推送 |
| 版本面冲突的 rebase 恢复 | `libra reset --mixed HEAD^` 撤销未推送的 C 组提交并清空暂存区，四个版本面文件恢复为原基线后从生命周期第 3 步重来；工作区独占检查断言暂存区为空，所以漏掉这一步会在 C 组之前就失败 |
| 建 tag 之后 release 失败 | tag 不改写；先删除该版本的候选分支（尖端必须是本卡 `PUSHED_SHA`，删除后回读为 404），再在已有 tag 的版本之上 bump 到下一版本前滚修复，新版本用新的候选分支 |
| 第三段 `gh release create` 报错或建 tag 后中断 | 原样重跑第三段：远端 tag 已存在且指向本卡 SHA、对应 Release 也存在时只补记 `TAG` 并继续第四段；tag 指向别的提交，或只有 tag 没有 Release，都停止并报告，不改写 tag |
| 第一段之前的外部前置检查失败 | C 组提交已在本地、尚未推送：只停止并报告，不做 `blocked` 登记（计划目录推送脚本也会拒绝父提交不在远端 `main` 上的登记提交）；前置恢复后重跑检查与第一段 |
| 网站段二推送报错或提交后中断 | 原样重跑段二：已有本卡网站提交（工作区干净、`HEAD` 的提交说明是本卡的同步说明）时不再提交，`origin/cf` 已等于它即视为推送成功；`origin/cf` 前进到别处时停止并报告：以 `origin/cf` 为底重新生成本卡页面，用 `libra commit --amend -S --no-edit` 重签（`libra rebase` 逐字节复制旧签名头，复制来的签名已失效；段二重跑时核对父提交变化时签名块也已变），取得新的审查 PASS 后再重跑 |
| 第三段发现更高版本 tag 或 CDN 已更新 | 不建 tag；本卡版本号作废并记入「实施证据」，在新基线上以新的 C 组提交前滚 |
| 候选分支 run 被取消或失败 | 第二段只看 `release/v<version>` 上本卡 SHA 的 run：被取消时 `gh run rerun` 一次，失败时 `gh run rerun --failed` 一次；重跑后仍不是 success 转修复模式（生命周期第 6 步）；超时保持 `in-progress` / `remote-pending`，原样重跑第二段继续等待 |
| 推送 `main` 前远端 `main` 上有其它提交的在途 run | 发布第一段或计划目录推送脚本停止，不推送（推送会让 `cancel-superseded-main` 取消它们，被取消的 run 不算 PASS）；等那些 run 结束后原样重跑；`gh` 查询或读取提交失败同样停止，恢复后重跑 |
| 第四段的降级演练失败 | 候选分支已删除、`RELEASE_RUN` 已写入，卡保持 `in-progress` / `remote-pending`，不写 `ROLLBACK_VERIFIED`；第四段可原样重跑（release run 与候选分支只核对，已删除的候选分支不再删除），按报错修正前置（网络、`/Volumes/Data/tmp` 空间、上一发布版本的 artifact）后重跑；演练 `PATH` 仍能找到 `libra` 或安装器未写入演练目录时停止，查明原因后重跑 |

## 风险登记

| 风险 | 处理 |
|---|---|
| 其它 `:` 形式被误切 | AC 锁住 `HEAD:path` 与 show 的 `:/` |
| 文档只改一边 | 每卡写集含对应命令的三份文档 |
| 写集扫描长期阻塞 | 按 DEP-I1055-07 如实保持 `pending` 并向用户报告，不放宽扫描 |

## 非目标与延后项

| ID | 内容 | 原因 |
|---|---|---|
| DEFER-I1055-01 | POSIX ERE 与大小写无关 | 现有正则方言已文档化 |
| DEFER-I1055-02 | #537 的其它 cat-file 旗标 | 不属于切分 |
| DEFER-I1055-03 | ls-tree 的 `<rev>^{tree}` 等类型剥离落到旧式导航，列出父提交的树（`src/command/ls_tree.rs:233-247` 的严格解析器只接受能剥成提交的说明，`src/utils/client_storage.rs:2928` 的未锚定 `(\^|~)(\d*)` 把 `^{tree}` 当成 `^1`） | 与冒号切分无关，修复需要改 ls-tree 的 treeish 解析入口（例如改用 `util::resolve_tree_ish_typed`），超出本计划；新 issue 授权时重启 |

## 完成判据

- [ ] I1055-01、I1055-02、I1055-03 都 `done`/`complete` 且各自有 T-1；I1055-01 的 TC-3285 两轨 PASS 记入「实施证据」。
- [ ] `plan-status.md` 本计划行同步；第六节 DEP-I1055-1057-LSTREE、DEP-I1053-1054-539、DEP-I1050-1057-REL 不由本计划的提交更新，由 `plan-status.md` 的例行同步维护；交接状态以本文件的卡字段为准，其它计划的开工门直接读取卡字段。
- [ ] 计划收口全量：最后一张卡的 C 组运行器在 bump 后的最终树上执行的 `cargo +nightly fmt --all --check`、`cargo clippy --all-targets --all-features -- -D warnings`、`source .env.test && source .env.live-test && cargo nextest run --all --no-fail-fast --retries 2` 与 release 构建就是本计划的收口全量，结论记在该卡「实施证据」的 C 组收据里；其后的 `remote` 与 `terminal` 提交只改本文件与 `plan-status.md`（暂存守卫与提交后范围检查保证），C 组结论携带也只在上游增量不被测试与构建读取时成立，所以终止链的树与 C 组验证过的树只差不被测试与构建读取的计划文件，不另跑一次，也不另开提交。C 组中任一失败先在写集内修复再整套重跑至绿；既有失败须在 bump 前的基线上复现，并登记 FIX- 或 DEFER-（ER-13）。
- [ ] 每张发布卡的 `Rollback mode` 已实际验证：发布四段第四段的降级演练成功，「实施证据」有该卡的 `<CARD>:ROLLBACK_VERIFIED=` 行（值为演练装上并核对的上一发布版本）。
- [ ] 关闭 issue 留给维护者。
